Summarization bias is hypothesized to operate in two regimes: (i) a generative regime, in which a model asked to render an emotion through Objective Projection defaults to declaring it instead; and (ii) an evaluative regime, in which a model judging narrative quality rewards told-mode explicitness and under-detects shown-mode suppression. The evaluative regime is the more consequential, since LLMs increasingly serve as judges and reward models, and a directional bias toward told mode would impose a selection pressure degrading prose toward flat declaration.
  This report does not claim the bias is validated. It defines the construct, situates it against LLM-as-judge biases, rereads a completed independent reliability study as directional evidence consistent with it, and pre-registers a two-regime test with decision rules under which the construct would be abandoned.

</details>


### [167] [On-Demand Attention: Language Models Know When to Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20734)
*Haibo Feng,Ruiqi Liang,Hanyang Peng,Shiqi Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 On-Demand Attention (ODA)，一种局部优先的长上下文解码方法，通过轻量级"召回头"预测全局注意力带来的收益，仅在收益变化时选择性调用全局注意力，从而在不改动预训练权重的前提下大幅减少全局读取并加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理与智能体工作负载日益需要高效的长上下文推理，但全注意力解码在每一步都会读取不断增长的历史，无论其对下一次预测是否有益，造成计算浪费。

Method: 作者发现预训练模型的解码状态在全局读取之前就已包含"全局注意力是否有益"的预测信息。基于此，ODA 采用局部优先解码，训练一个轻量级召回头（recall head）来监测预测收益的变化，并据此选择性触发全局注意力；仅训练召回头而冻结预训练权重，完整历史 KV 缓存得以保留以供未来召回。此外在 vLLM 中实现 GPU 端的条件执行，将减少的全局读取转化为实际的长上下文解码加速。

Result: 在 Qwen 和 Gemma 模型（含混合注意力骨干）上的实验表明，选择性召回能恢复局部注意力损失的大部分性能，同时显著减少全局读取，并获得长上下文下相对全注意力的实际解码加速。

Conclusion: 研究支持这样一种长上下文推理范式：让预训练模型自主引导对其所保留信息的访问，在性能与效率之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Reasoning and agentic workloads increasingly demand efficient long-context inference. Yet full-attention decoding reads the growing history at every step, regardless of its benefit to the next prediction. We show that a pretrained model's decoding states already contain information predictive of this benefit, before the global read. Building on this finding, we introduce On-Demand Attention (ODA), a local-first decoding method that uses a lightweight recall head to selectively invoke global attention as its predicted benefit changes during generation. ODA trains only the recall head, leaving pretrained weights unchanged and the complete historical KV cache available for future recall. We further implement GPU-side conditional execution in vLLM, translating reduced global reads into practical decoding speedups over full attention at long context lengths. Experiments across Qwen and Gemma models, including hybrid-attention backbones, show that selective recall recovers most of the performance lost under local attention while substantially reducing global reads. These findings support long-context inference in which pretrained models guide their own access to the information they retain.

</details>


### [168] [dQwen3.5: Hybrid-Attention Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20751)
*Anton Xue,Litu Rout,Aditya Akella,Adam Klivans,Sujay Sanghavi,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨将混合注意力-RNN架构的预训练自回归模型（Qwen3.5，0.8B–9B）适配为扩散语言模型（dQwen3.5族），证明混合骨干网络是高效起点：达到同等训练损失仅需约一半token，且在任意顺序解码与并行解码上表现接近全注意力DLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的适配几乎都从全注意力Transformer出发，但自回归建模已转向注意力与RNN层交错的混合架构。RNN在结构上具有因果性、难以双向化，这种不匹配构成了适配障碍。作者希望探究混合骨干网络能否仍被有效适配为扩散语言模型。

Method: 以Qwen3.5在0.8B、2B、4B、9B四个规模上开展适配实验，得到dQwen3.5模型族；并以全注意力Transformer为对照，比较达到给定训练损失所需token数量、任意顺序解码行为以及并行解码下的性能。

Result: 混合骨干网络能达到给定训练损失所需的token约为全注意力对照的一半，效率显著更高；dQwen3.5在任意顺序解码行为上与全注意力扩散语言模型相似，且在并行解码下表现强劲，且该结果在四个规模上均成立。

Conclusion: 混合注意力-RNN架构可以成为扩散语言模型的高效预训练起点，打破了此前仅依赖全注意力骨干的局限；即使RNN层具有结构因果性且难以双向化，仍可通过适配获得与全注意力DLM相当的解码能力。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model is a cost-efficient route to a diffusion language model (DLM). While nearly all such adaptations start from a full-attention transformer, AR modeling has shifted toward hybrid architectures that interleave attention and RNN layers. This creates an obstacle for adaptation: unlike attention, RNNs are structurally causal and nontrivial to bidirectionalize. Despite this mismatch, we investigate whether such backbones can become effective DLMs by adapting Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B scales, yielding the dQwen3.5 family. We find that hybrid backbones can be efficient starting points for adaptation: against a full-attention control, the hybrid reaches a given training loss in about half the tokens. Across scales, dQwen3.5 resembles full-attention DLMs in any-order decoding behavior and performs strongly under parallel decoding.

</details>


### [169] [Harm Laundering in GPT Models: Evidence That Gender Discrimination Is Transformed Rather Than Reduced Across Safety-Trained Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20779)
*Sarah Wyer,Sue Black,Noura Al Moubayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文指出，基于表面形式分类器（toxicity 分数）的LLM安全评估系统性不完整：显式歧视内容只是被"转化"而非被消除，作者称之为"危害洗白"（harm laundering）。通过对 OpenAI GPT 系列 15 个模型、45 万条性别导向补全的分析发现，随着模型迭代，毒性分数下降，但表征性危害反而上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 安全评估依赖表面形式分类器，报告逐代下降的"危害分数"。作者质疑这一方法论是否真的反映了危害的减少，怀疑模型只是将显式歧视换成了更隐蔽的偏见形式，导致评估给出虚假的安全感。

Method: 分析 45 万条性别导向补全，覆盖 GPT-2 至 GPT-5 的 15 个模型（OpenAI GPT 谱系），并设置三种人口学条件。采用主题聚类（如 Topic 5 共 1,997 篇文档）、三个独立毒性分类器评分、情感分数、主题多样性比（W/M），以及 REGARD 表征危害指标与 Detoxify 毒性指标和发布日期的相关性分析（Spearman ρ）。提出"危害洗白"的三准则检验法，并给出适用于任意生成式模型的三阶段检测协议。

Result: GPT-2 中指向女性的性暴力主题簇在 GPT-4 时消失，但指向男性的补全获得了女性补全所没有的正面表征领域（照料、情感广度、盟友身份）；GPT-5 时 Topic 5（1,997 篇文档）将乳腺癌框定为"男性权利"议题，而女性导向输出中无对应簇。三个独立分类器均将该内容评分为无毒。情感分数在 GPT-4 反转：早期模型贬低女性，后期模型则过度纠正。女性导向补全的主题多样性在 GPT-4 对齐边界处相对男性下降 36%（W/M=0.58，GPT-2 时为 0.91）。REGARD 表征危害差异与发布日期正相关（ρ=+0.55，p=.034），而 Detoxify 不相关（ρ=-0.23，p=.42）：毒性分数下降的同时表征危害在增长。

Conclusion: 在 OpenAI GPT 谱系中，毒性分数的下降并不能充分代理危害的减少。安全评估必须超越表面形式分类器，纳入对表征性危害（如偏见的内容迁移与主题垄断）的检测，否则会遗漏"危害洗白"这一系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for large language models rely on surface-form classifiers that report declining harm scores across model generations. We provide evidence that this methodology is systematically incomplete: explicit discriminatory content is transformed rather than removed. We call this \emph{harm laundering}. Analysing 450,000 gender-directed completions across 15 models spanning GPT-2 through to GPT-5 (OpenAI GPT lineage; three demographic conditions), we show that sexual violence clusters prevalent in GPT-2 women-directed output disappear by GPT-4, while men-directed completions gain positive representational territory (caregiving, emotional range, ally identity) that women-directed completions do not. The pattern is most visible at GPT-5: Topic~5 (1,997~documents) frames breast cancer as a men's rights debate, while zero equivalent clusters appear in women-directed output. Three independent classifiers score this content as non-toxic. Sentiment scores invert at GPT-4: early models demean women; later models over-correct. Topic diversity in women-directed completions falls 36\% relative to men at the GPT-4 alignment boundary (W/M~$= 0.58$, from $0.91$ at GPT-2). REGARD representational harm disparity correlates with release date ($ρ= +0.55$, $p = .034$) while Detoxify does not ($ρ= -0.23$, $p = .42$): toxicity scores fall as representational harm grows. We formalise harm laundering as a three-criteria test and provide a three-stage detection protocol applicable to any generative model. Within the OpenAI GPT lineage, toxicity score reduction is not a sufficient proxy for harm reduction.

</details>


### [170] [RetireOPD: Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20784)
*Yan Yu,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Yichen Pan,Aozhe Wang,Qipeng Chen,Hua Yang,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RetireOPD（自适应退出的在线策略蒸馏）方法，通过强化学习优化技能条件教师，再联合 RL 与在线策略蒸馏训练技能无关学生，并在适当时机自动停止使用教师，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 任务上显著超越 RL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体通过强化学习每轮轨迹仅获得单一标量奖励，监督信号稀疏，因此易采用在线策略蒸馏（OPD）从具备特权任务技能的教师处获得逐词元监督。但研究发现两个问题：其一，仅靠特权信息并不能保证教师可靠；其二，教师监督的收益具有阶段依赖性，需要在合适时机停用教师。

Method: RetireOPD 分两步：先用环境奖励优化一个解耦的、技能条件化的教师；再联合 RL 与 OPD 训练技能无关学生。其关键创新是自适应退隐（Adaptive Retirement），不使用预定义蒸馏计划，而是当学生与教师之间的差异不再缩小、且学生成功率达到教师目标比例时，让学生自行停止依赖教师，之后仅用 RL 继续训练。

Result: 在 1.5B 至 7B 的 Qwen2.5 模型上，RetireOPD 在 ALFWorld 上相较 RL 基线成功率提升 14.1% 至 18.8%，在 WebShop 上准确率提升 11.8% 至 19.0%，并在所有设置上超越了其自身的技能条件教师。

Conclusion: RetireOPD 通过优化可靠教师并引入自适应退隐机制，克服了特权教师不可靠及监督收益阶段依赖性的问题，实现了比 RL 基线和技能条件教师更优的智能体性能。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) receive a single scalar reward per trajectory, which motivates self on-policy distillation (OPD) to supply dense token-level supervision from a self-teacher with privileged task skills, letting a skill-free student internalize them. This recipe, however, is undermined by two findings in agentic tasks: privileged information alone does not always make a teacher reliable, and the benefit of teacher supervision is stage-dependent. We therefore propose RetireOPD (Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation), which first optimizes a decoupled, skill-conditioned teacher with environment rewards and then trains a skill-free student jointly with RL and OPD. Rather than following a predefined distillation schedule, RetireOPD adopts Adaptive Retirement: the student drops the teacher on its own once their discrepancy stops shrinking and it reaches a target fraction of the teacher's success rate, after which training proceeds with RL alone. Across Qwen2.5 models from 1.5B to 7B, RetireOPD improves ALFWorld success rate over RL baseline by 14.1% to 18.8% and WebShop accuracy by 11.8% to 19.0%, and surpasses its own skill-conditioned teacher in every setting.

</details>


### [171] [JEPA-Anything: Learning Predictive Models across Different Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20800)
*Taoyong Cui,Zhongyao Wang,Xinyue Xu,Weiyang Liu,Zhaochen Yu,Yuying Zhang,Qiang Gao,Mengyue Yang,Wanli Ouyang,Pheng Ann Heng,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 JEPA-Anything，一个基于正交预测分解（OPF）的领域无关世界建模框架，将潜在目标分解为互补因子并通过专用通路学习后重组，在七个领域（视觉、生物、临床轨迹、控制、分子动力学、物理场、天气）验证了其跨域通用性，在多项动力学任务、干预预测、长时程分子模拟及科学发现上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型多为领域专属，作者追问是否存在一种通用学习原理能支撑跨完全不同系统的世界建模，并进一步将世界建模与干预和实验验证的科学发现联系起来。

Method: 引入 JEPA-Anything 框架，基于正交预测分解（OPF）。OPF 是对联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的扩展：将潜在目标分解为互补因子，通过专用通路分别学习各因子，再在共享预测设计内重组。

Result: 在全部10个匹配动力学任务上超越匹配的 JEPA 基线；在 Interventional Pong 上把单次干预预测误差降低 34.8%；在四个分子系统中取得最低的一步和100步分子误差。此外，因子提名的生物干预在细胞共培养、患者源性类器官、肿瘤片段和小鼠中得到实验支持；潜在轨道模式恢复开普勒缩放指数，拟合斜率 -1.4991。

Conclusion: 结果表明一种共通的分解式预测原理可跨异构世界成立，将世界建模与干预及有实验依据的科学发现相连接，验证了领域无关世界建模的可行性。

Abstract: World modeling enables intelligence to anticipate consequences, guide interventions, and learn from interaction. Yet predictive models remain domain-specific: can a common learning principle support world modeling across radically different systems? We introduce JEPA-Anything, a domain-agnostic framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization (OPF). Extending joint-embedding predictive architectures, OPF decomposes latent targets into complementary factors, learns them through dedicated pathways, and recombines them within a shared predictive design. We evaluate JEPA-Anything across seven domains: vision, biology, clinical trajectories, control, molecular dynamics, physical fields, and weather. Experiments span representation learning, intervention prediction, out-of-distribution generalization, and long-horizon dynamics, including 10 matched dynamics tasks, forecasting of over 1,000 clinical events, and 100-step molecular rollouts across four systems. Against matched JEPA baselines, JEPA-Anything improves reported metrics on all 10 dynamics tasks and reduces single-intervention prediction error on Interventional Pong by 34.8%. It achieves the lowest one-step and 100-step molecular errors among compared methods in all four systems. Beyond prediction, a factor-nominated biological intervention receives experimental support in cell co-cultures, patient-derived organoids, tumor fragments, and mice; latent orbital modes recover the Keplerian scaling exponent with a fitted slope of -1.4991. These results support a common factorized predictive principle across heterogeneous worlds, connecting world modeling with intervention and experimentally grounded scientific discovery. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/JEPA-Anything

</details>


### [172] [Unifying Models of Intergroup Hostility in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20808)
*Patrick Gerard,Julia Mendelsohn,Kristina Lerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用2024年美国大选期间来自TikTok、Truth Social和Twitter/X的286万条帖子，在统一实证框架内量化社会与道德心理学、政治学中关于群体间敌意的六种基础理论机制（边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊化、负面评价、去人性化、行动导向），揭示其在真实话语中的结构与时间顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对群体间敌意修辞的现有理论主要在社会心理学、道德心理学和政治学中并行发展，各自提出不同甚至相互冲突的解释，且极少在真实话语中互相对照检验，导致对敌意修辞机制的理解碎片化，缺乏对其在现实话语中表现及相互关系的清晰认识。

Method: 以2024年美国总统选举期间TikTok、Truth Social、Twitter/X三个平台的286万条帖子为数据，在统一实证框架下对六种基础理论机制（边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊化、负面评价、去人性化、行动导向）进行建模，从结构层面（网络/共现关系）和时间层面（时间排序）恢复群体间敌意修辞的整体组织方式。

Result: 结构上发现边界建构与威胁建构是整个体系的锚定（核心）机制；时间上发现各机制呈现规律性的先后顺序：边界建构、贬损与行动导向倾向于较早出现，去人性化与威胁建构较晚出现，替罪羊化最晚出现。

Conclusion: 通过绘制各理论框架在真实话语中的实际表现，弥合了社会科学各传统之间长期存在的分歧，并为计算社会科学提供了一种超越单一标签检测的、更清晰的群体间敌意修辞建模实证基础。

Abstract: Hostile rhetoric toward social groups can normalize exclusion and justify mistreatment, as well as contribute to rising polarization and political violence. Efforts to moderate hostile rhetoric in online speech draw on foundational theories in social and moral psychology, and political science. However, these theories were developed largely in parallel, often propose different and sometimes conflicting accounts of how hostility develops, and have rarely been tested against each other in real discourse. The result is a fragmented understanding of the rhetorical mechanisms of hostility, without a clear sense of how they appear, and relate to each other, in real-world discourse. Using 2.86 million posts from TikTok, Truth Social, and Twitter/X during the 2024 U.S. presidential election, we model the mechanisms of six foundational theories of intergroup hostility -- boundary construction, threat construction, scapegoating, negative evaluation, dehumanization, and action orientation -- within a common empirical framework to recover the broader organization of intergroup hostility rhetoric. Structurally, we find that boundary construction and threat construction anchor the system; temporally, we find that these mechanisms tend to follow a regular ordering: boundary construction, derogation, and action orientation tend to appear early; dehumanization and threat construction later; scapegoating latest. Mapping how these theoretical frameworks actually manifest in discourse bridges longstanding divisions across social science traditions and presents computational social science with a clearer empirical foundation for modeling intergroup hostility rhetoric beyond single-label detection.

</details>


### [173] [Embedding Models Measure in Peculiar Ways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20821)
*Juri Opitz,Andrianos Michail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入空间中语义相似度是否反映物理测量（质量、距离、时间、体积）的客观等价关系，发现物理测量仅被弱建模，且嵌入表征受表面字符串相似度的强烈影响，相似度重校准也难以改善对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理测量（质量、距离、时间、体积）具有唯一、客观的语义等价与距离定义，可作为理想基准来检验嵌入空间是否真正编码了语义相似度而非表面特征。作者希望借此揭示嵌入模型的语义表征能力及其潜在偏差。

Method: 选取具有客观等价关系的物理量（质量、距离、时间、体积）作为测试维度，分析词嵌入空间中这些测量概念的表示，考察其语义距离模式是否与物理测量一致；进一步探究表面字符串相似度对表征的影响，并通过相似度重校准（recalibration）尝试改善嵌入与物理测量之间的对齐。

Result: 物理测量在嵌入空间中仅被弱建模，反而呈现出相当奇特的测量模式；嵌入表征受表面字符串相似度的强烈影响；对相似度进行重校准并不能实质性改善与物理测量的对齐。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间并未很好地反映物理测量这一客观的语义等价关系，其表征更多受表面形式（字符串相似度）驱动，说明嵌入模型对深层语义的编码能力存在局限，且简单的相似度校准无法弥补这一缺陷。

Abstract: Embedding spaces define notions of semantic similarity and distance. We study whether those embeddings reflect physical measurements of mass, distance, time and volume, which admit a unique, objective notion of semantic equivalence and distance. We find that physical measurement is only weakly modeled in the embedding space, and that instead quite peculiar measurement patterns can be observed. Further analysis indicates that embedding representations of physical measurements are strongly influenced by superficial string similarity, and recalibration of similarity does not substantially improve the alignment.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [An absolute measure of heterogeneity for lnOR in meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19211)
*Ke Yang,Lei Shi,Wangli Xu,Liping Zhu,Tiejun Tong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于二分类结局 Meta 分析的新异质性度量 ICC_MA^OR，并给出其估计量 I²_A，该度量与样本量无关，克服了传统 I² 随样本量增大而趋近于 1 的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的异质性统计量 I² 严重依赖研究样本量，样本量增大时趋于 1，导致解读失真；已有的样本量无关度量 ICC_MA 仅适用于连续型结局的 Meta 分析，无法直接用于实践中更常见的二分类结局（以 lnOR 为效应量）。

Method: 作者引入潜连续变量模型，在以 lnOR 为效应量的随机效应 Meta 分析框架下，推导出具有闭式表达的新异质性度量 ICC_MA^OR，并提出新估计量 I²_A 对其进行估计。

Result: 模拟研究表明，新估计量 I²_A 几乎无偏，且当连续变量可观测时，与用于标准化均值差的对应度量高度吻合。真实数据应用显示，针对二分类结局的异质性统计量与连续结局的对应度量跨结局类型提供了可解释且一致的估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的 ICC_MA^OR 及估计量 I²_A 为二分类结局 Meta 分析提供了样本量无关、可解释且与连续结局度量一致的异质性量化工具，填补了相关方法空白。

Abstract: Quantifying the heterogeneity is an important issue in meta-analysis. The conventional heterogeneity statistic, $I^2$, heavily depends on study sample sizes and tends to approach one as the sample sizes increase. To overcome this limitation, a sample size independent measure, ${\rm ICC}_{\rm MA}$, has recently been developed to quantify the heterogeneity, yet this method is restricted to meta-analysis with continuous outcomes. In practice, however, binary outcomes are more widely encountered, with the log odds ratio (lnOR) frequently adopted as an effect size to compare the effects of two treatments in terms of odds. To fill the gap, we propose a new heterogeneity measure for meta-analysis with binary outcomes by introducing a latent continuous variable model. Under this framework, the proposed measure admits a closed-form expression within the random-effects meta-analysis model with lnOR as the effect size. We define this sample size independent measure as ${\rm ICC}_{\rm MA}^{\rm OR}$, and moreover propose a new estimator, $I^2_A$, to evaluate this measure. Simulation studies demonstrate that our new estimator is nearly unbiased and aligns closely with its counterpart applied for standardized mean differences when the continuous variable is observable. A real data application further demonstrates that the proposed heterogeneity statistic for binary outcomes and its counterpart for continuous outcomes provide interpretable and coherent estimates across different outcome types.

</details>


### [175] [When a High Score Is an Illusion: Certifying Genuine versus Repackaged Forecasting Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19223)
*Pin Ni,Francesca Medda,Ramin Okhrati*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究排名对比分析中基准观测复用导致的关联问题，刻画了保持指定总体排名对比关联的基准赋值条件；提出无偏三轨迹核估计、U统计量与共享基线区间等方法，并通过北京空气质量数据的经验研究证实在多种设定下复用会显著夸大预报与结果的共享得分与拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排名依赖于用于比较的观测值，复用这些观测会在被评估的预报和结果保持固定时，也在预报排名与结果排名的对比之间引入虚假关联。作者旨在刻画当参考基准被重新赋值时，能够保持特定总体排名对比间关联不变的确切条件，以避免复用基准带来的统计偏差。

Method: 在独立训练条件下，整条轨迹从共同分布中独立抽样，轨迹内部允许不受限的依赖。将期望得分分解为目标项与映射对之间的显式交互项；在保持已学习映射、参考分布和系数行和不变的约束下重新赋值参考，证明每个映射对的参考重叠权重为零是均匀保持关联的充要条件。提出无偏的三轨迹核估计交互项，利用独立评估与验证提供有限样本下界；在全部抽样可重组合时用完全U统计量直接估计同一目标；构造含并列情形的尖锐共享基线区间以收紧估计。另在类别拟合误差与时间反馈、配对类别控制零假设实验等设定下分别检验。

Result: 在北京空气质量档案中，交互项占七个学习预报在经验档案分布下期望共享得分的91.4%至94.5%。在配对类别控制零假设实验中，采用不同参考将使1,000个面板中的拒绝次数从402降至41。

Conclusion: 复用参考观测会在排名对比中引入实质性的关联，进而显著夸大预报的共享得分与统计显著性。通过强制零加权参考重叠、采用独立轨迹估计和共享基线区间，可以在有限样本下无偏且更紧地估计真实目标，从而避免因基准复用导致的虚假结论。

Abstract: Ranks depend on the observations used for comparison. Reusing those observations can add association between forecast and outcome rank contrasts even when the evaluated forecast and outcome stay fixed. We characterize assignments that preserve association between specified population-rank contrasts, including forecast rank minus baseline rank compared with outcome rank minus baseline rank. Conditional on independent training, whole trajectories are sampled independently from a common law, with unrestricted dependence within each trajectory. The expected score separates into its target and an explicit interaction between map pairs. When reassigning references, we keep the learned maps, reference law and coefficient row sums fixed. Zero weighted reference overlap for every map pair is necessary and sufficient for preservation uniformly over permitted maps and laws. An unbiased three-trajectory kernel estimates the interaction; independent evaluation and validation provide finite-sample lower bounds. Complete U-statistics estimate the same target directly when all draws can be recombined. Sharp shared-baseline ranges, including ties, tighten both constructions. In a Beijing air-quality archive, interaction accounts for 91.4% to 94.5% of seven learned forecasts' expected shared scores under the empirical archive law. Separate results address category-fitting error and temporal feedback. In a matched category-control null experiment, distinct references reduce rejections from 402 to 41 out of 1,000 panels.

</details>


### [176] [Binning-Independent Bayesian Analysis of Time-Dependent Perturbed Angular Distribution data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19227)
*Franziskus von Spee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无分箱（binning-free）似然函数的贝叶斯方法来分析时间依赖的微扰角分布（TDPAD）数据，用于提取原子核的 g 因子，且在低统计量数据下比传统分箱方法更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的时间依赖微扰角分布（Time-Dependent Perturbed Angular Distribution, TDPAD）数据分析通常需要将时间数据分箱，这会损失信息并在低统计量情形下引入偏差或不确定性。作者希望避免时间分箱，直接利用单事件的概率信息，从而更充分地利用数据、提高低统计量数据下 g 因子提取的可靠性。

Method: 作者利用单事件的条件概率构建似然函数，从而完全避免了时间数据的离散化分箱。随后将该似然函数放入贝叶斯框架中，计算所关注的 g 因子的后验概率密度函数（posterior probability density function）。为验证方法，作者用模拟数据集将其与使用分箱数据的传统方法进行了对比。

Result: 分析模拟数据发现，所得后验概率密度在很多情况下呈多峰（multimodal）分布，因此往往不能用单一高斯近似来概括结果。对比表明，新提出的方法在低统计量数据集上给出的结果比传统分箱方法更可靠。

Conclusion: 该无分箱、基于单事件条件概率的贝叶斯方法能更可靠地处理低统计量的 TDPAD 数据。由于后验分布常为多峰，作者强调不应简单用单一高斯近似来表征结果，应采用能反映多峰结构的完整后验密度。

Abstract: We propose a new approach for analyzing Time-Dependent Perturbed Angular Distribution data. In this, a likelihood is constructed with the help of conditional probabilities of single events and binning of time data is avoided. This likelihood is used in a Bayesian framework to calculate the posterior probability density function for the $g$ factor of interest. This approach is compared to more traditional approaches that use binned data by analyzing simulated datasets. In many cases the resulting posterior probability densities are observed to be multimodal and the results can thus often not be summarized with a single Gaussian approximation. We find that results from the new approach are more reliable for low-statistics datasets.

</details>


### [177] [Laplacian U-Processes for Multiple Change-Point Detection in Dependent Text Networks: An Application to Historical Chinese Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19306)
*Fanghua Chen,Yizhou Cai,Lu zhou,Ting Fung Ma*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双视图加权共现算子（WCO）的离线变点检测框架，用于弱依赖文本网络；通过词共现图的一阶直接共现与二阶共享上下文视图，配合WCO扫描与两类自助法，检测跨视图依赖变化并定位变点，并给出渐近理论保证及《新青年》语料实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本网络（如词共现图）中检测弱依赖情形下的变点，需要同时利用一阶直接共现与二阶共享上下文信息，以捕捉跨视图依赖结构的变化；现有方法在弱依赖网络中理论保证和定位精度有限，需要一个兼具非参数有效性与理论严密性的框架。

Method: 提出双视图加权共现算子（WCO）框架：用加权词共现图构造一阶直接共现与二阶共享上下文两个视图，经独立试点语料学习到的映射投影为配对欧氏观测；WCO扫描检测跨视图依赖变化；采用依赖乘子自助法（dependent multiplier bootstrap）检验是否存在变点；用分段块自助法（segment-wise block bootstrap）给出变点位置的描述性稳定性区间；并以CUSUM和高斯核MMD扫描作为互补基准。

Result: 建立了原假设下的弱收敛、自助法有效性、渐近水平控制、单一可识别变点下的一致性定位，以及词元级误差的扰动界；在1915–1921年《新青年》语料中检测到1919年11月的变点，描述性95%稳定性区间为1919年4月至1920年6月，与五四运动及新文化运动的语言转型相符。

Conclusion: 所提WCO框架能够有效检测弱依赖文本网络中的变点且具备理论保证，实证结果与历史语言学转型（五四/新文化运动）一致，验证了方法的实用性与解释力。

Abstract: We propose a two-view weighted-concordance operator (WCO) framework for offline change-point detection in weakly dependent text networks. Weighted word-co-occurrence graphs yield first-order direct-co-occurrence and second-order shared-context views, projected into paired Euclidean observations using maps learned from an independent pilot corpus. A WCO scan detects changes in cross-view dependence. A dependent multiplier bootstrap tests for a change, while a segment-wise block bootstrap gives a descriptive stability interval for its location. We establish null weak convergence, bootstrap validity, asymptotic size control, consistent localization under a single identifiable change, and perturbation bounds for token-level errors. CUSUM and Gaussian-kernel MMD scans provide complementary benchmarks. In the 1915-1921 New Youth corpus, the method detects a change in November 1919, with a descriptive 95 percent stability interval from April 1919 to June 1920, consistent with the linguistic transition surrounding China's May Fourth and New Culture movements.

</details>


### [178] [When are time-to-event models a waste of time? Bridging mixture cure models and positive-unlabeled learning for binary classification under right-censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19370)
*Qin Weng,Matthew M. Engelhard*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文分析了混合治愈模型（MCMs）与正-无标签（PU）学习两种框架在处理删失数据下二分类任务的关系，并通过模拟与真实临床队列证明：仅在无法识别删失机制特征的选择性删失情境下，MCM优于PU学习；其余情况下PU学习性能相当且可避免时间-事件（TTE）方法的挑战与偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中预测二分类结局常受右删失干扰，无法区分事件永不发生（阴性/非易感）与删失后发生（阳性/易感）的个体。MCM虽能同时建模结局概率与事件时间分布，但依赖可能不可靠、昂贵或有偏的事件时间戳；PU学习则把无观测事件者归为"无标签"。作者旨在厘清两类框架的关系，为临床实践提供证据驱动的选择建议。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明两类模型族优化相同的似然，差异在于对易感患者在删失前发生事件概率（即PU学习中的"标签倾向"）的约束方式不同；MCM可视为将标签倾向参数化为TTE分布的PU模型，从而具有可识别性。随后通过系统模拟及两个真实临床队列，考察加入TTE成分是否及何时能提升二分类性能。

Result: 研究发现MCM仅在一种特定情境下占优：存在选择性删失且无法识别决定删失的确切特征时。在其他情况下，PU学习达到与MCM相当的性能，同时避免了TTE方法带来的挑战与潜在偏倚。

Conclusion: 应以删失机制是否可被特征可识别为关键判别标准来选择方法：除非面临不可识别的选择性删失，否则推荐优先采用PU学习，因其性能不劣于MCM且更稳健、成本更低、偏倚风险更小。

Abstract: In clinical settings, predicting binary outcomes is often complicated by right-censoring, which prevents us from distinguishing individuals in whom the event never occurs (i.e., negative, or non-susceptible) from those where it occurs after the censoring time (i.e., positive, or susceptible). To discriminate between these subpopulations, we can use mixture cure models (MCMs), which model both the outcome probability over an infinite horizon and its time-to-event (TTE) distribution when observed. However, the TTE approach requires event timestamps, which can be unreliable, costly to obtain, or biased due to disparities in clinical care. Alternatively, the task can be formulated as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, in which individuals without an observed event are grouped as "unlabeled" rather than negative, recognizing that this group includes positives whose event occurred after censoring. Here we analyze the relationship between these frameworks and provide evidence-based recommendations for choosing between them. We begin by showing that both families optimize a shared likelihood but differ in how they constrain the probability of event occurrence prior to censoring among susceptible patients, which in PU learning is known as the labeling propensity; and that an MCM may be viewed as a PU model whose labeling propensity is parameterized as a TTE distribution, which makes the model identifiable. In systematic simulations and two real-world clinical cohorts, we examine whether and when including the TTE component benefits binary classification. We show that MCMs are preferred in only one specific regime: under selective censoring where the exact features governing censoring cannot be identified. Otherwise, PU learning achieves equivalent performance while avoiding challenges and potential biases associated with the TTE approach.

</details>


### [179] [Qualify-Then-Borrow: A Five-Step Framework for Bayesian Borrowing Beyond Outcome Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19382)
*Haitao Pan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出 Qualify-Then-Borrow (QTB) 方法，在动态贝叶斯借用前先对外部信息进行科学评估并分类（合格、可修复、不合格），再决定直接使用、修复或排除，最后对剩余的残差经验兼容性进行动态借用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态贝叶斯借用方法仅依据当前数据与外部数据的一致性来调整外部信息贡献，但缺乏对外部数据质量的事先科学评估，可能导致不合适的借用决策与偏差。

Method: QTB 采用五步架构：定义目标；评估外部数据；将其分类为合格、可修复或不合格；分别直接使用、修复后使用或排除；最后应用所选的贝叶斯借用方法进行动态借用。通过 82 个预设场景、每个场景 10,000 次重复进行模拟评估。

Result: 仅凭结局数据无法确定正确的借用决策；两个外部结局分布相同的场景产生相似的稳健混合行为但 QTB 决策不同。借用错误分类数据时，完全合并使 95% 覆盖率降至 60.8%，稳健混合降权将覆盖率提升至 91.8% 但仍有 -4.8 个百分点偏差。修复测量的总体偏移将绝对偏差从 1.94 降至 0.80 个百分点。在强负残差漂移下，I 类错误率达 10.0%。数据完全兼容时 QTB 退化为常规稳健混合分析。

Conclusion: QTB 是一种五步决策架构，而非新的先验或兼容性统计量；它在动态借用前决定外部信息是原样使用、需修复还是排除，再对残差经验兼容性进行借用以降低偏差。资格认定并不保证可交换性，降权只是保障而非对残差偏差的保证。

Abstract: PURPOSE: Dynamic Bayesian borrowing methods adapt the contribution of external information according to agreement or disagreement with current data. Qualify-Then-Borrow (QTB) makes explicit how scientific source assessment determines what external information reaches the borrowing model and when observed outcome agreement should determine borrowing strength.
  METHODS: QTB uses five steps: define the target; assess the external data; classify them as qualified, repairable, or not qualified; use them directly, repair them before use, or exclude them; and then apply the selected Bayesian borrowing method. Dynamic borrowing is the final step and operates on residual empirical compatibility after identified material differences have been addressed. We evaluated 82 prespecified scenarios with 10,000 replications each.
  RESULTS: Outcome data alone could not determine the appropriate borrowing decision. Two settings with the same external outcome distribution produced similar robust-mixture behavior but different QTB decisions because one had a known endpoint problem. When misclassified data were borrowed, full pooling reduced 95% coverage to 60.8%; robust-mixture downweighting improved coverage to 91.8% but left a -4.8 percentage-point bias. Repair of a measured population shift reduced absolute bias from 1.94 to 0.80 percentage points. Under strong negative residual drift, Type I error reached 10.0% despite downweighting. With fully compatible data, QTB reduced to the usual robust-mixture analysis.
  CONCLUSION: QTB is a five-step decision architecture, not a new prior or compatibility statistic. It determines whether external information proceeds unchanged, requires repair, or is excluded before dynamic borrowing operates on residual empirical compatibility. Qualification does not certify exchangeability, and downweighting is a safeguard rather than a guarantee against residual bias.

</details>


### [180] [Omitted Variable Bias in Difference-in-Differences Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19386)
*Juejue Wang,Pedro H. C. Sant'Anna,Victor Chernozhukov,Carlos Cinelli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究双重差分（DiD）设计中的遗漏变量偏误（OVB）问题，提出了一种新的ATT偏误公式，并基于混淆强度提出了灵敏度统计量、边界方法及利用机器学习的统计推断，最后以最低工资对青少年就业的实证为例验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在典型DiD设计中，未观测混杂会导致平行趋势假设被违背，从而产生遗漏变量偏误。现有方法缺乏对这一偏误的清晰刻画，也缺乏可操作的工具来量化需要多强的混淆才能推翻研究结论。

Method: 推导处理组平均处理效应（ATT）遗漏变量偏误的新颖公式；将偏误主要归因于处理分配机制中的混淆强度，并提出三种量化方式（处理组中处理的平均几率变化、处理组与对照组的混淆不平衡、未处理组处理几率中被解释的变异）；基于此构建灵敏度统计量以报告推翻结论所需的最小混淆强度，并通过与观测协变量或前趋势比较给出混淆强度的正式边界；最后提出灵活高效的统计推断方法，可利用现代机器学习算法估计ATT边界。

Result: 给出了ATT偏误的显式刻画及其由混淆强度支配的结论；提供了可供常规报告的灵敏度统计量和基于协变量/前趋势比较的正式边界；开发了可结合机器学习的灵活高效推断方法；在最低工资对青少年就业影响的实证中展示了该方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文为DiD设计中的遗漏变量偏误提供了理论刻画和实用的灵敏度分析工具，使研究者能够量化结论对未观测混杂的稳健性，并借助现代机器学习方法实现灵活高效的边界推断。

Abstract: We study the omitted variable bias (OVB) problem in canonical difference-in-differences (DiD) designs when unobserved confounding induces departures from the parallel trends assumption. Our results provide a novel characterization of the OVB formula for the average treatment effect on the treated (ATT), which is of independent interest. We show how the ATT bias is mainly governed by the strength of confounding in the treatment assignment mechanism and provide alternative ways of quantifying this strength, such as (i) changes in the average odds of treatment among the treated, (ii) confounding imbalance between treated and control units, or (iii) variation explained in treatment odds among the untreated. Building on these results, we offer sensitivity statistics for routine reporting, describing the minimum strength of confounding required to overturn the conclusions of a DiD study, as well as formal bounds on the strength of confounders based on comparisons to observed covariates or pre-trends. Finally, we provide flexible and efficient statistical inference methods for the bounds on ATT, which can leverage modern machine learning algorithms for estimation. We demonstrate the utility of our approach in an empirical example that estimates the effects of minimum wage on teen employment.

</details>


### [181] [Bayesian Sample Size Determination: Sampling Distribution Estimation or Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19489)
*Luke Hagar,Paul Gustafson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从理论上推导了贝叶斯样本量确定（Bayesian SSD）中各类方法的蒙特卡洛抽样分布，并通过大量数值研究比较了"抽样分布估计"与"随机根搜索探索"两类策略在偏差、精度和对调参敏感性方面的表现，以促进贝叶斯样本量确定的最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 很多贝叶斯研究需选择使检验功效足够或区间估计足够窄的样本量，但对于复杂模型，确定最小合适样本量通常需要密集的蒙特卡洛模拟。现有多种模拟策略（估计后验摘要的抽样分布 vs. 通过随机根搜索探索），其性能评估依赖于样本量推荐的蒙特卡洛抽样分布，但这些分布此前缺乏理论刻画与系统比较。

Method: 本文针对多种贝叶斯样本量确定方法，从理论上推导其样本量推荐的蒙特卡洛抽样分布；并开展大规模数值研究，估计"抽样分布估计法"与"探索法"这两类策略的蒙特卡洛抽样分布，评估其偏差、精度及对调参参数的敏感性。

Result: 得到各类贝叶斯样本量确定方法样本量推荐的蒙特卡洛抽样分布的理论结果，并通过数值研究量化了两类策略在偏差、精度和调参敏感性方面的表现差异。

Conclusion: 论文为贝叶斯样本量确定提供了理论工具与实践指导，有助于研究者依据偏差、精度和稳健性选择更合适的样本量确定策略，从而推广贝叶斯样本量确定的最佳实践。

Abstract: To design many Bayesian studies, the sample size is chosen to attain sufficient power to reject a null hypothesis or a desired probability that an interval estimate is sufficiently narrow. Determining the minimum suitable sample size for studies with complex models typically requires intensive Monte Carlo simulation. One simulation-based strategy to consider the sample-size space involves estimating sampling distributions of posterior summaries, such as posterior probabilities or interval estimate lengths, at various sample sizes. Another strategy instead explores these sampling distributions via stochastic root finding over the sample-size space, where the entire sampling distribution of posterior summaries is not estimated at any given sample size. The performance of these competing approaches can be assessed using their Monte Carlo sampling distributions of sample size recommendations across repeated implementations. In this paper, we theoretically derive these Monte Carlo sampling distributions for various Bayesian sample size determination approaches. To promote best practices for Bayesian sample size determination, we conduct extensive numerical studies to estimate these Monte Carlo sampling distributions for the sampling distribution estimation and exploration approaches to assess their bias, precision, and sensitivity to tuning parameters.

</details>


### [182] [Spatial Aggregation of ROC and Precision-Recall Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19517)
*Romain Pic,Zhongwei Zhang,Sebastian Engelke,Johanna Ziegel*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究空间场型二值事件预报中ROC与PR曲线的聚合策略，提出满足“支配保持”与“凹性/可实现性保持”的两种聚合方法，并给出解释聚合曲线的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气预报中预报以空间场形式给出，逐位置计算的ROC与PR曲线常需聚合以便比较，但聚合策略如何影响预报辨别力评估尚不清楚，缺乏能保证公平比较的聚合准则。

Method: 作者对ROC与PR曲线的不同聚合策略进行理论分析，识别出满足两个理想性质（保持预报间的支配关系、保持曲线凹性或可实现性）的条件，给出充分条件并提出两种满足条件的聚合策略，与文献中现有策略进行比较并剖析其潜在陷阱。

Result: 存在聚合策略会破坏预报间支配关系或曲线凹性/可实现性，导致误导性解释；文章找到满足上述性质的充分条件并提出两种新策略，证明其满足公平比较要求，同时用基于AI的全球天气预报实例展示不同聚合策略会产生不同的预报排名。

Conclusion: 聚合策略会显著影响基于ROC与PR曲线的辨别力评估与预报排名，应选择满足支配保持与凹性/可实现性保持的策略；文章据此为聚合曲线的解释提供实用指南，并用实例验证了策略选择的重要性。

Abstract: Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) and Precision-Recall (PR) curves are widely used to assess the discrimination ability of forecasts for binary events, such as threshold exceedances or warnings of extreme events. In weather forecasting, forecasts are provided as spatial fields, yielding location-wise ROC and PR curves that are often aggregated to facilitate comparison. However, the effect of the aggregation strategy on performance assessment remains poorly understood.
  We investigate how different aggregation strategies for ROC and PR curves affect the assessment of discrimination ability. In particular, we identify conditions under which aggregation strategies satisfy two desirable properties for fair comparison: preservation of dominance between forecasts and preservation of concavity or achievability of the curves. We obtain sufficient conditions and propose two strategies satisfying them. They are compared with existing strategies from the literature, and we analyze their properties and highlight potential pitfalls that may lead to misleading interpretations. Based on these findings, we provide practical guidelines for the interpretation of aggregated ROC and PR curves. The proposed framework is illustrated with AI-based global weather forecasts, showing how different aggregation strategies can yield different rankings of competing forecasts.

</details>


### [183] [Vector Vine Copula Models for Multivariate Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19547)
*Michael Stanley Smith,Lin Deng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出向量可绘藤（VD-vine）copula，将传统可绘藤 copula 从标量节点推广到向量节点，用于建模具有非高斯边缘、非线性动态和各波次响应向量组成变化的多变量纵向数据，并通过贝叶斯变分推断实现高效似然评估与预测模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量纵向数据常呈现非高斯边缘分布、非线性动态依赖性，且不同波次的响应向量组成可能不同；传统方法难以同时刻画这些特征。

Method: 提出 VD-vine copula：每个波次的响应向量构成一个多元边缘，波次间的序列依赖通过一系列链接向量 copula 捕捉。证明 VD-vine 本身是向量 copula 且在标量节点时退化为传统可绘藤；推导前向与后向条件传输用于高效似然评估和预测模拟，并给出有限阶马尔可夫与平稳约束下的精简形式。采用高斯和 FGM 链接向量 copula 的无约束参数化、灵活的多元边缘以及贝叶斯变分推断实现。

Result: 模拟显示当边缘分布不对称且序列依赖为多元时预测精度更高，而在正确设定的高斯面板向量自回归下几乎无损。在澳大利亚八波次 1,093 人、响应向量变化的纵向数据中，完整 VD-vine 在所有比较模型中取得最佳交叉验证分布预测性能。

Conclusion: VD-vine 能够有效刻画纵向数据中的不对称性与非线性依赖，带来更优的分布预测。

Abstract: Multivariate longitudinal data may exhibit non-Gaussian margins, nonlinear dynamics, and response vectors with composition that varies across waves. To account for these features, we introduce a vector drawable vine (VD-vine) copula that extends conventional drawable vine copulas from scalar to vector-valued nodes. Here, the response vector at each wave forms a multivariate marginal, and serial dependence is captured through a sequence of linking vector copulas. We establish that the VD-vine is itself a vector copula and reduces to a conventional drawable vine for scalar nodes. Recursive forward and backward conditional transports are derived that enable efficient likelihood evaluation and predictive simulation, with parsimonious reductions under finite-order Markov and stationary restrictions. Unconstrained parameterizations for Gaussian and FGM linking vector copulas, flexible multivariate marginals, and Bayesian variational inference provide a practical implementation. Simulations show improved predictive accuracy when the marginals are asymmetric and serial dependence is multivariate, with little loss under a correctly specified Gaussian panel vector autoregression. In an eight-wave Australian panel of 1,093 individuals with varying response vectors, the full VD-vine delivers the best cross-validated distributional forecasts among the models considered, establishing the benefit of capturing asymmetry and nonlinear dependence.

</details>


### [184] [Oracle high-dimensional $M$-estimation using smooth reparameterization for sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19558)
*Yoichi Nishiyama*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的高维M估计非线性正则化框架，将稀疏性直接嵌入参数重参数化映射RePS中，而非使用传统非凸惩罚，在高维线性模型和Cox比例风险模型中同时实现优异的假阳性控制与覆盖率，并建立oracle等价性与渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统加性非凸惩罚（如SCAD、MCP）在高维优化中面临严重的计算与理论困难，且难以统一处理线性模型与生存分析模型；作者希望用光滑重参数化的方式将稀疏性直接编码进参数变换，从而规避非凸优化的同时保留无偏估计和oracle性质。

Method: 提出基于光滑（C²）逐分量“稀疏性重参数化映射（RePS）”φ^(ν)的框架，令物理参数 θ=φ^(ν)(β)，惩罚项为 λ‖β‖₁（而非 λ‖θ‖₁）；该方法能随局部参数尺度自适应地抑制高维噪声，并利用Primal-Dual Witness方法在线性模型与Cox比例风险模型下统一建立oracle等价性，适用维度相关的对数尺度条件 n^{-1/2} ≪ ν ≤ log p，并显式容纳生存分析中的平移不变性等模型特定结构。

Result: 在样本量趋于无穷时，框架恢复无偏的oracle渐近正态性（oracle asymptotic normality）；蒙特卡洛模拟显示，在高维线性模型中该方法持续取得更优的假阳性控制与更高的95%置信区间覆盖率，在比例风险模型中其综合表现与SCAD、MCP等最先进方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该统一RePS正则化框架为高维线性模型和Cox模型提供了一种既有理论保障（oracle等价性与渐近正态性）、又有数值优势的稀疏估计方案，克服了加性非凸惩罚的优化难题，并通过重参数化设计实现了对局部尺度的自适应。

Abstract: This paper establishes a unified non-linear regularization framework for high-dimensional $M$-estimation, encompassing both linear models and Cox's proportional hazards models. Rather than relying on traditional additive non-convex penalties which pose severe optimization challenges, the proposed paradigm embeds sparsity directly into the transformation for the physical parameter $θ=φ^{(ν)}(β)$ using a smooth ($C^2$) component-wise "ReParametrization map for Sparsity (RePS)" $φ^{(ν)}$, and the penalty term is $λ\Vert β\Vert_1$ rather than $λ\Vert θ\Vert_1$. This structural formulation dynamically adapts to local parameter scales, suppressing high-dimensional noise while simultaneously recovering unbiased oracle asymptotic normality under the large-sample limit. Through the Primal-Dual Witness method, we establish a unified oracle equivalence for both model classes under a dimension-dependent logarithmic scaling $n^{-1/2} \ll ν\leq \log p$, explicitly accommodating model-specific structures such as the shift-invariance in survival analysis. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves superior false-positive control and high 95% confidence interval coverage in high-dimensional linear models, while also delivering performance comparable or superior in all aspects to state-of-the-art methods like SCAD and MCP in proportional hazards models.

</details>


### [185] [Selective Inference in Growth Curve Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19573)
*Haochen Lei,Qian Zhang,Hongyuan Cao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据裂变（data-fission）框架的成长曲线模型选择后推断方法，通过在数据中加减高斯噪声将选择阶段与推断阶段的信息分离，在保留全部被试的同时，为所选模型中的协方差加权线性投影参数提供有效（渐近或精确）的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成长曲线模型在心理学研究中被广泛使用，变量选择有助于识别与纵向异质性相关的基线特征。然而，由于同一份结果数据既用于变量选择又用于推断，传统的数据驱动选择后推断可能无效（产生偏差）。因此需要一种在数据被重复使用情况下仍能保证推断有效性的方法。

Method: 作者提出一种数据裂变框架：对原始数据分别加上和减去高斯噪声，将信息划分为用于变量选择的部分和用于推断的部分，同时两个阶段均保留全部被试。该框架可容纳灵活的变量选择程序，并将推断目标设定为所选工作模型中的协方差加权线性投影参数。当协方差结构已知时提供精确推断；当协方差结构需估计时，在适当正则条件下建立渐近有效性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法能够提供有效的推断，并且相比被试水平的数据分割（subject-level data splitting）具有更高的效率；而朴素的选择后推断（naïve post-selection inference）可能存在偏差。对美国青年纵向研究（Longitudinal Study of American Youth）的应用展示了该框架的实际可用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据裂变框架为成长曲线模型中的数据驱动变量选择后推断提供了一种有效且高效的解决方案，既避免了朴素方法的选择偏差，又相比简单数据分割保留了更多统计效率，具有理论保证并在实际数据中得到验证。

Abstract: Growth curve models are widely used in psychological research, and variable selection can help identify baseline characteristics associated with longitudinal heterogeneity. However, conventional inference after data-driven variable selection can be invalid because the same outcome data are used for both selection and inference. We develop a data-fission framework for post-selection inference in growth-curve models that separates the information used for selection and inference while retaining all participants in both stages through the addition and subtraction of Gaussian noise. The framework accommodates flexible variable-selection procedures and targets covariance-weighted linear projection parameters in the selected working model. It provides exact inference when the covariance structure is known, and we establish asymptotic validity under suitable regularity conditions when the covariance structure is estimated. Simulations show that the proposed method provides valid inference and improves efficiency over subject-level data splitting, whereas naïve post-selection inference can be biased. An application to the Longitudinal Study of American Youth illustrates the proposed framework.

</details>


### [186] [Heterogeneity-calibrated Byzantine-robust distributed composite quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19701)
*Xiaofei Wu,Jian Qing Shi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究异构诚实站点与拜占庭工作节点构成的分布式数据下稀疏复合分位回归问题，提出"异质性校准稳健复合分位回归"（HC-RCQR）。该方法在本地将截距边际化（profile）并用近似逆 profile Hessian 校准得分后上传，服务器端通过坐标截断与软阈值迭代更新，从而在存在恶意消息时实现条件收缩与支持恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中站点间高度异构（协变量分布、误差律、分位截距均可不同），且部分工作节点可能是发送任意向量的拜占庭节点。现有稳健估计方法难以同时应对这种统计异质性与对抗性污染，且异质性本身会放大拜占庭攻击的影响，因此需要能校准异质性并抵御污染的稀疏分位回归方法。

Method: 提出 HC-RCQR：① 诚实站点本地对截距做 profile 处理，并利用近似逆 profile Hessian 对得分向量进行校准；② 上传校准后的向量；③ 拜占庭节点可发送任意向量；④ 服务器端对收到的向量做坐标维截断（coordinatewise trimming）与软阈值（soft thresholding）更新估计量。通过一个标量示例揭示不等曲率下中间值拜占庭报告如何穿过截断存活、以及理想校准如何压缩其影响；并证明在故障身份未知时，无约束诚实站点斜率均值不可识别。

Result: 在适当条件下建立了条件收缩与支持恢复保证，误差界将得分偏移、抽样波动、污染、校准误差与牛顿余项五项分离。标量示例量化了异质曲率下拜占庭攻击的残余影响及校准的削减作用；模拟与共享单车需求数据分析验证了方法在异构数据与对抗消息下的表现。

Conclusion: HC-RCQR 通过本地截距 profile 化与基于近似逆 profile Hessian 的得分校准，有效控制了分布式异构场景下拜占庭污染的影响；理论上的条件收缩和支持恢复保证以及配套实验均支持该方法在异构、对抗环境下的稳健性与实用性。

Abstract: We study sparse composite quantile regression (CQR) for distributed data with heterogeneous honest sites and Byzantine workers. Honest sites share a common slope but may differ in their covariate distributions, error laws, and quantile intercepts. The proposed heterogeneity-calibrated robust CQR (HC-RCQR) profiles local intercepts and calibrates scores using an approximate inverse profile Hessian. Honest workers transmit the resulting vectors, whereas Byzantine workers may send arbitrary vectors. The server updates the estimate by coordinatewise trimming and soft thresholding. A scalar example shows how unequal honest-site curvatures allow intermediate Byzantine reports to survive trimming and how ideal calibration reduces their possible effect. We also establish nonidentification of the mean of unrestricted honest-site slopes when fault identities are unknown. Under suitable conditions, we establish conditional contraction and support-recovery guarantees. The bound separates score offset, sampling fluctuation, contamination, calibration error, and the Newton remainder. Simulations and a bike-demand study examine performance under heterogeneous data and adversarial messages.

</details>


### [187] [Report resolution in federated multiple testing under family-wise error control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19708)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究多个机构在不共享数据的前提下进行家族假设检验时，只能发布有限分辨率（离散化）的 p 值报告。文章量化了这种报告相对最优集中式程序（oracle）的检验功效损失，核心结论是损失源于压缩而非去中心化；最优损失随分辨率的平方反比衰减，等宽区间即可达到这一最优阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个机构需在族误差控制下检验一组假设，但因隐私或制度限制无法汇集原始数据，只能由各站点对每个假设发布离散化的 p 值报告。作者希望量化这种有限分辨率报告造成的功效损失，厘清损失究竟来自"去中心化"还是"压缩"，并寻找能达到最优功效的报告方式与合并规则。

Method: 将问题形式化为"报告设计 + 合并规则"的联合优化，以最强大的集中式程序（oracle）为基准，在给定的正则性与功效目标条件下分析功效损失。通过理论分析证明损失随分辨率平方反比衰减，并证明等宽区间划分可达到该最优阶（任何等单元格数的划分都不能更优）。同时给出给定报告下的功效最优合并规则，以及一个仅利用报告在零假设下精确已知分布、且在任意跨假设相关性下均有效的合并规则；并以双站点 Beta 分布例子进行数值验证。

Result: 在所述条件下，任何有限分辨率都会损失功效，且最优损失按分辨率平方的倒数衰减，等宽区间达到该阶且无法被同单元数划分超越。数值实验中，双站点 Beta 例子里优化的 1 比特报告对单假设几乎无损，但对两个假设的功效损失数倍于单假设；在 0.05 水平、两个 Beta(1,2) 假设下，最优合并的三比特等宽报告保留了 oracle 功效的 99.2%。

Conclusion: 量化确认了功效损失来自 p 值报告的压缩而非去中心化本身；有限分辨率必然带来损失，但可通过合理设计使损失按分辨率平方反比地快速衰减（等宽区间即可达到最优阶），在适中的分辨率下即可逼近 oracle 功效，为分布式且受限报告场景下的多重检验提供了理论与实用指导。

Abstract: Several institutions test one family of hypotheses under family-wise error control but cannot pool their data, so each site releases, for each hypothesis, only a report of its own p-value. The report's resolution is the number of values it can take. We quantify the power lost to such reports relative to the most powerful centralized procedure (the oracle), with reports and combining rule chosen optimally. A report from which the p-value can be recovered loses no power, so the loss is due to compression, not decentralization. Under the stated regularity and power-objective conditions, every finite resolution loses power, and the optimal loss decays as the inverse square of the resolution: equal-width intervals attain this order, and no partition into as many cells improves it. In two-site Beta examples, optimized one-bit reports are nearly lossless for a single hypothesis but lose several times as much power for two. At level 0.05 with two Beta(1, 2) hypotheses, optimally combined three-bit equal-width reports retain 99.2% of oracle power. We give the power-optimal combining rule for given reports, and a rule using only their exactly known null distribution, valid under arbitrary dependence across hypotheses.

</details>


### [188] [Matrix Graphical Model Via Joint Estimation of Partial Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19718)
*Hyewon Kim,Seongoh Park*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对矩阵图模型（可分离协方差假设下的矩阵变量条件依赖结构）提出一种偏相关的联合估计方法，在统一优化框架下同时估计所有偏相关，从而保持估计图的对称性并简化调参，在数值实验和肺结核蛋白质表达数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵图模型通过将精度矩阵分解为Kronecker乘积，可分别对行、列域的无向图建模。现有方法中，基于似然的方法直接估计精度矩阵、间接恢复图结构；基于回归的方法直接估计变量间连边，性能更优，但依赖多个带惩罚的回归问题，导致估计的图缺乏对称性，且调参困难。因此需要一种能保持对称性、简化模型选择的联合估计方法。

Method: 提出矩阵图模型中偏相关的联合估计方法，将所有偏相关置于统一的优化框架内同时估计，从而保持估计结果的对称性，并减轻调参负担（通过联合优化而非多个独立的惩罚回归问题）。

Result: 数值研究表明，所提方法相比现有方法提升了图恢复性能。在肺结核患者蛋白质表达数据（多个时间点重复测量）的应用中，该方法比较了两组患者的蛋白质网络，并恢复了时间依赖结构。

Conclusion: 联合估计偏相关的方法有效解决了回归类方法的不对称性与调参难题，并在图结构恢复精度上优于现有方法，适用于真实生物医学数据的组间网络比较与时间依赖结构建模。

Abstract: Matrix graphical models aim to characterize conditional dependence structures in matrix-variate data under a separable covariance assumption. In this framework, the precision matrix is decomposed as a Kronecker product, enabling separate modeling of undirected graphs across row and column domains. Existing methods have been developed for this problem, including likelihood-based approaches and regression-based procedures for graph estimation. Likelihood-based methods estimate precision matrices directly and recover graph structures indirectly, whereas regression-based approaches directly target estimating edges among variables, thus outperforming the former. However, existing regression-based methods are based on multiple penalized regression problems, which naturally yields asymmetry in estimated graphs and computational difficulty in selecting tuning parameters. To address the limitations, we propose a joint estimation of partial correlations in matrix graphical models. The proposed method estimates all partial correlations simultaneously within a unified optimization framework, thereby preserving symmetry and easing the pain of selecting the best models. Numerical studies demonstrate that the proposed method improves graph recovery performance compared to existing approaches. We also analyze protein expression data collected from patients with pulmonary tuberculosis, measured repeatedly at multiple time points, where the proposed method compares protein networks between two groups of patients and recovers the temporal dependence structure.

</details>


### [189] [Mutual Information as a Tool for Optimal Classification: Application to Identifying Rapid-Responding Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19781)
*Santeri Holopainen,Jari Metsämuuronen,Mikko-Jussi Laakso,Janne V. Kujala*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非参数、基于互信息的框架，用于在大规模测评中识别快速作答行为，无需对总体分布作参数假设，并通过PISA 2022数学数据验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别大规规模测评中快速作答行为的方法需要对总体分布作参数假设，作者希望提供一种非参数的替代方案，以避免参数假设带来的局限。

Method: 提出基于互信息的非参数框架：计算观察作答与离散化反应时间之间的互信息，并通过最大化信息增益确定区分快速作答与投入作答的阈值。框架内各方法的差异仅在于变量分类数。文中具体给出三种方法：方法一使用作答正确性与二分化反应时间；方法二使用正确性并将时间分为三类；方法三使用原始作答与二分化时间。

Result: 将三种方法应用于PISA 2022数学成就数据，并考察了方法一在若干现实条件下的行为与可用性。结果表明，所提框架是识别快速作答的可行替代方案，其新颖性在于非参数特性及能够利用原始作答（而非仅正确性）。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为识别快速作答行为的可行替代方法，具有非参数、可利用原始作答等优势；最后讨论了未来可能的研究方向。

Abstract: Existing methods for identifying rapid-responding behaviour in large-scale assessments require parametric assumptions about the population. In this study, we propose a novel, non-parametric, mutual information-based framework of methods as an alternative. The methods within this framework compute the mutual information of the observed responses and discretised response times and maximise the information gain to determine a threshold that differentiates rapid responses from engaged responses. The only difference between the methods is the number of categories in the relevant variables. We present three methods explicitly. The first method uses response correctness and binarised response times. The second method uses correctness and categorises time into three groups. The third method uses raw responses and binarised times. We applied these methods to mathematics achievement data collected through the Programme for International Student Assessment in 2022. Furthermore, we examined the behaviour and usability of the first method in certain realistic conditions at the population and realised levels. The results indicated that the proposed framework is a viable alternative for identifying rapid responses. The framework's novelty lies in its non-parametric nature and its ability to utilise raw responses instead of correctness. Finally, we discuss some possible future research directions on this topic.

</details>


### [190] [Multi-Absorbing Phase-Type Distributions for Right-Censored Competing Risks Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19921)
*Zhihao Qiao,Budhi Surya,Azam Asanjarani,Yoni Nazarathy*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出多吸收相型（MAPH）分布用于竞争风险模型，开发了处理精确事件时间/原因及独立右删失数据的EM算法，并配套发布两个Julia软件包，在重症监护住院时长数据上取得比以往方法更高的似然值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相型（PH）分布是用于寿命时长的灵活半参数模型，在生存与可靠性分析中应用广泛，但竞争风险框架下（需同时记录吸收时间及其原因）缺少统一的参数化模型、推断算法与软件实现，且删失数据的处理尤为困难。

Method: 提出多吸收相型（MAPH）分布，同时刻画吸收时间与吸收原因；推导其基本性质；开发EM算法，对精确事件时间/原因观测与独立右删失观测进行参数推断。删失个体的E步骤以生存至删失时刻为条件，同时填补潜隐瞬态路径与最终吸收原因，并利用矩阵指数得到原因分解的充分统计量期望的闭式解。配套实现两个Julia软件包。

Result: 在完整（含删失记录）的重症监护住院时长数据上，该方法获得了比先前发表的相型竞争风险拟合更高的似然值；并通过应用与数值性质说明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MAPH分布及其EM算法为竞争风险场景下的相型建模提供了完整可行的统计推断与软件工具，能有效利用删失信息并提升拟合效果，在生存与可靠性分析中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Phase-type (PH) distributions are versatile semi-parametric models for lifetime duration and can be used in survival and reliability analysis. In this paper we put forward methods and software for using PH distributions in a competing-risks model. The resulting multi-absorbing phase-type (MAPH) distribution records both the time until absorption and its cause. After formulating basic properties of this family, we develop an EM algorithm for parameter inference from exact event-time/cause observations and independently right-censored observations. For a censored subject, the E-step conditions on survival up to the censoring time and imputes both the latent transient path and its eventual cause of absorption; closed-form cause-resolved sufficient-statistic expectations are obtained from matrix exponentials. We illustrate applications and numerical properties and describe two accompanying Julia packages, in which both the exact-event and the censored E-step are implemented. Applied to intensive-care length-of-stay data in full, censored records included, the method attains a higher likelihood than the phase-type competing-risks fit previously published for those data.

</details>


### [191] [Gain-function optimisation of graphical multiple testing procedures for confirmatory clinical trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19994)
*Alexander D. V. Spiers,Michael J. Grayling,Graham M. Wheeler,Adrian P. Mander*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过最大化显式“收益函数”来选择图形化多重检验程序的方法，使多重检验策略更好地反映试验实际目标，同时保证家族错误率控制，并支持处理设计阶段假设的不确定性及群序贯设计中的早期申领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形化多重检验程序虽灵活且能控制家族错误率，但实践中申办方往往依赖固定序列或Holm程序，这些预设图形可能无法准确反映试验真实目标；如何选择图形这一关键问题缺乏系统化的、以目标为导向的方法。

Method: 提出选择使显式收益函数最大化的图形，收益函数刻画每种被拒绝假设集合对申办方的价值（通常在越过监管门槛前价值为零，之后每个额外申领带来增量收益）。传统功效指标可视为其特例。设计一套从临床试验、商业和监管团队成员处引出收益函数的结构化流程。通过在设计阶段对处理效应和相关性取一组合理值并对其上的性能加权平均来处理不确定性；对群序贯设计，进一步引入对早期申领的奖励机制。

Result: 五个来自真实及假设性制药试验的示例表明：最优图形取决于“试验成功”的定义方式、设计假设的不确定程度，以及在群序贯设计中申领能够确立的时间点。由于搜索限定在图形化程序内，所选图形成立时仍能控制家族错误率。

Conclusion: 基于显式收益函数优化选择图形化多重检验程序，能够使统计策略更贴合试验的临床、商业和监管目标，并在控制家族错误率的前提下，系统整合假设不确定性及早期申领价值，优于依赖单一预设图形的传统做法。

Abstract: Graphical multiple testing procedures are a flexible and transparent way to control the family-wise error rate when a confirmatory trial pursues several label claims, but they leave open the question of which graph to use. In practice sponsors often fall back on fixed-sequence or Holm procedures that may poorly reflect what the trial is actually trying to achieve. We propose to instead choose the graph that maximises an explicit gain function, which states what each possible set of rejected hypotheses is worth to the sponsor: typically nothing until a regulatory hurdle is cleared, then an incremental amount for each further claim. Conventional power criteria are recovered as special cases, and because the search is confined to graphical procedures, family-wise error rate control holds whichever graph is selected. We set out a structured procedure for eliciting the gain function from the clinical, commercial, and regulatory members of a trial team. The proposed framework also handles uncertainty in the treatment effects and correlations assumed at the design stage, by averaging performance over their plausible values rather than fixing a single assumption. For group sequential designs, we describe how it can further reward early claims. Five examples, drawn from real and hypothetical pharmaceutical trials, show that the best graph depends on how trial success is defined, how uncertain the design assumptions are, and, in a group sequential setting, when claims can be established.

</details>


### [192] [A Bayesian Bi-Directional Splitting Framework for Variable Selection in Large Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20000)
*Aaron Coats,Vinny Davies,Mayetri Gupta*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对现代表格数据"样本多且协变量多"的双高维场景，本文提出一种基于分而治之的贝叶斯变量选择框架，将数据沿行与列两个方向切块并行独立分析，再用两阶段共识过程合并结果，在保留变量选择性能的同时显著降低计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表格数据在样本数和协变量数上同时快速增长，给贝叶斯变量选择带来巨大计算负担。已有文献大多只单独解决"大样本"或"高维协变量"其一，鲜有工作在实际场景中同时处理两个维度都很大的可扩展贝叶斯变量选择问题。

Method: 提出一种新的贝叶斯变量选择框架，采用分而治之策略：沿行方向和列方向同时将数据分割成多个批次，各批次并行独立分析，随后通过一个两阶段共识（consensus）过程将各批次结果合并，得到统一的变量选择结果。

Result: 实验表明该方法获得显著的计算效率提升，同时在含噪高维场景下仍保持较强的变量选择性能，即便数据分割造成信息损失，也能成功识别相关信号。论文还给出了调参选择和有效数据划分策略的实践指南，并在 H3N2 流感数据集上进行了实际应用验证。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了样本与协变量双高维情形下贝叶斯变量选择的可扩展性问题，在计算效率与选择精度之间取得良好平衡，并通过实验与实践指南证明了方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Modern tabular datasets are becoming increasingly large, both in the number of samples and covariates, posing significant challenges for Bayesian variable selection due to the resulting computational burden. While there is extensive literature on scaling Bayesian inference to large numbers of observations or high-dimensional covariate spaces, comparatively little work addresses scalable Bayesian variable selection when both dimensions are large simultaneously in a practical setting. This paper presents a novel Bayesian variable selection framework for efficiently analysing data with a large number of both rows and columns. The proposed framework operates via a divide-and-conquer approach, splitting data into batches along both directions and analysing each batch independently in parallel, after which the results are combined together in a two-phase consensus procedure. Experiments demonstrate the computational gains while retaining strong variable selection performance, successfully identifying relevant signals in noisy, high-dimensional settings despite the loss of information induced by data splitting. Practical guidelines are also provided, including recommendations for tuning parameter choices and effective data partitioning strategies, followed by a real application to an H3N2 influenza dataset.

</details>


### [193] [Parameter Estimation for the Mixed Fractional Merton Jump Diffusion Model with EM Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20041)
*Chidiogo Joy Agboeke,Hamidreza Maleki Almani,Dario Gasbarra,Foad Shokrollahi,Tommi Sottinen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Metropolis–Hastings采样的期望最大化算法，用于估计混合分数Merton跳跃扩散模型的参数，并将模型应用于赫尔辛基股指（OMXH25）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融收益率时间序列同时呈现长期记忆依赖（由分数布朗运动刻画）和突发跳跃（由复合泊松过程刻画）两种特征，传统模型难以同时有效捕捉这两类行为，需要一种可靠的参数估计框架。

Method: 提出混合分数Merton跳跃扩散模型，结合分数布朗运动（捕获长程依赖）与复合泊松跳跃过程（描述收益的突变）。采用含Metropolis–Hastings采样的期望最大化（EM）算法进行参数估计：E步利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样推断潜在跳跃过程，M步通过最大化期望完全数据似然更新模型参数。

Result: 在适当的正则条件下，证明了所提估计量的一致性与渐近正态性；将该方法应用于赫尔辛基股指（OMXH25），提供了可靠且计算高效的估计框架，能够同时建模长期记忆依赖与跳跃行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的估计框架为同时具有长记忆依赖与跳跃行为的金融时间序列建模提供了一种可靠且计算高效的方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes an Expectation--Maximization algorithm with Metropolis--Hastings sampling for parameter estimation in a Mixed Fractional Merton Jump Diffusion model. The model combines fractional Brownian motion to capture long-range dependence with a compound Poisson jump process to describe abrupt movements in financial returns. The latent jump process is inferred during the E-step using Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, while the M-step updates the model parameters by maximizing the expected complete-data likelihood. The consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed estimator are established under suitable regularity conditions. The methodology is applied to the Helsinki Stock Index (OMXH25). The proposed estimation framework provides a reliable and computationally efficient approach for modeling financial time series exhibiting both long-memory dependence and jump behavior.

</details>


### [194] [Noise-adjusted turnover in estimated networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20044)
*Sultan Amed,Sayantan Banerjee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究两期网络快照的Hamming翻转量（网络边集变化）在存在图选择误差时的统计性质，在齐次边误分类模型下给出基于观测翻转量和两期估计图规模的无偏调整公式，并分析稀疏性、校准误差与跨期依赖对偏差的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济网络通常分两个时期分别估计，并将边集变化解读为结构性重连。但由于两期网络都是估计得到的，观测到的边集变动不仅反映真实重连，还混入了图选择（估计）误差。作者旨在厘清这种误差如何污染翻转量度量，并给出可操作的修正与一致性条件。

Method: 在齐次边误分类模型下（已知灵敏度与特异度，且两期估计边指示器条件独立），推导两快照Hamming翻转量泛函的闭式无偏调整量，该调整仅依赖观测翻转量和两期估计图的大小。随后分析稀疏性、校准误差（误分类概率标定偏差）以及跨期（非条件独立）依赖的影响，并推导跨期依赖引起的偏差及相对网络规模一致性的充分条件，辅以数值模拟。

Result: 当真实连边数正比于p时，阶为p^{-1}的假阳概率会产生阶为p的期望伪翻转量；对假阳概率的校准误差若同阶，亦会在调整后留下阶为p的偏差。文章给出跨期依赖所致的偏差表达式，并给出相对网络规模一致性的充分条件。数值结果验证了有限样本下的理论含义。

Conclusion: 两期网络比较中，图选择误差会系统性地夸大结构化重连的测度；基于已知误分类概率和两期图规模的闭式调整可无偏修正观测翻转量，但其有效性对误分类概率的校准精度和跨期独立性条件敏感，需满足相应条件才能保证一致性。

Abstract: Economic networks are often estimated separately over two periods, and changes in their edge sets are interpreted as structural rewiring. Since both networks are estimated, observed turnover also reflects graph-selection error. We study the two-snapshot Hamming-turnover functional under a homogeneous edge-misclassification model. With known sensitivity and specificity and conditional independence of the estimated edge indicators across periods, latent turnover admits a closed-form unbiased adjustment based only on observed turnover and the two estimated graph sizes. We then examine the effects of sparsity, calibration error and dependence across periods. When the number of true links is proportional to $p$, a false-positive probability of order $p^{-1}$ generates expected spurious turnover of order $p$. An error of the same order in calibrating the false-positive probability can likewise leave order-$p$ bias after adjustment. We also derive the bias induced by cross-period dependence and give sufficient conditions for consistency relative to network size. Numerical results illustrate the finite-sample implications.

</details>


### [195] [Identification and Estimation of Causal Estimands with Missing Not at Random Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20113)
*Faria Rauf Ria,Tarikul Islam,Mahbub A. H. M. Latif*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在数据为"非随机缺失"（MNAR）且混杂因素与结局同时存在缺失的背景下，研究因果效应（平均处理效应）与中介效应（自然直接/间接效应）的识别与估计问题，提出基于完备性条件的识别结果和 EM 算法估计方法，并通过模拟与 NHANES 实证证明其偏倚显著低于完全案例分析和多重填补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果推断中，MNAR（非随机缺失）数据会同时影响混杂因素和结局变量，且在仅有因果推断所需常规假设的情况下，因果估计量通常不可识别。现有常用的完全案例分析和多重填补等方法在 MNAR 机制下往往产生较大偏倚，因此需要发展专门针对 MNAR 的识别理论与估计方法。

Method: 本文在若干合理的 MNAR 机制下，利用完备性（completeness）条件建立平均处理效应的非参数/半参数可识别性结果；在估计上，采用期望最大化（EM）算法处理缺失数据并估计因果估计量。随后将该识别与估计框架推广到中介分析，用于估计自然直接效应和自然间接效应。

Result: 大量模拟研究表明，在所考察的 MNAR 机制下，所提方法相比完全案例分析和多重填补两种常用方法，偏倚显著更低。将方法应用于 NHANES 数据，以健康状况为中介变量，估计了教育对抑郁的因果效应。

Conclusion: 通过在 MNAR 机制下引入完备性条件，可以实现平均处理效应及自然直接/间接效应的可识别，并借助 EM 算法给出有效估计；该方法在模拟和真实数据中均优于传统的缺失数据处理策略，为 MNAR 情形下的因果与中介分析提供了可行的理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Missing not at random (MNAR) data pose significant challenges for causal inference, particularly when both confounders and the outcome are partially observed. Without additional assumptions beyond those required for causal inference, causal estimands are generally not identifiable under MNAR mechanisms. This paper first develops identification results for the causal estimand, the average treatment effect, under several plausible MNAR mechanisms using completeness conditions, and proposes an estimation approach based on the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. We further extend this identification and estimation framework to mediation analysis, enabling the estimation of natural direct and indirect effects under MNAR mechanisms. Through extensive simulation studies, we compare the proposed method with two widely used approaches for handling missing data, complete-case analysis and multiple imputation. The results show that the proposed method yields substantially lower bias under the considered MNAR mechanisms. Finally, we apply the proposed approach to NHANES data to estimate the causal effect of education on depression, with health condition as the mediator.

</details>


### [196] [Exponential Smoothing for Time Series of Random Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20274)
*Takuo Matsubara,Peiwen Jiang,Wilson Ye Chen,Minh-Ngoc Tran*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出测地线指数平滑法，将指数平滑推广到Hadamard空间中的随机对象时间序列，通过单个标量参数沿测地线更新预测水平，并证明平滑参数估计量的几乎必然一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵、概率分布、函数型数据等随机对象时间序列无法依赖标准算术运算进行预测，需要一种不依赖标准运算、结构轻量且能在线更新的预测框架，以替代结构更重的对象值自回归模型。

Method: 提出测地线指数平滑法：预测水平沿测地线向每个新观测移动固定比例，平滑参数通过最小化观测与预测间的平均平方距离估计；并引入创新机制，使每个观测的条件Fréchet均值等于当前水平，构成度量空间中的创新状态空间模型。利用Hadamard空间的拟线性化建立生成过程的样本路径性质并证明估计量一致性。

Result: 该框架仅含单一标量参数，无需平稳性假设，每观测常数时间在线更新；在创新机制下建立样本路径性质并证明平滑参数估计量的几乎必然一致性。三个真实数据应用（协方差矩阵、分布型、函数型时间序列）验证了其预测性能优于结构更重的替代方法。

Conclusion: 测地线指数平滑为Hadamard空间中的对象值时间序列提供了一种简洁、在线、无平稳性假设且理论性质可证的有效预测方法，在实际应用中表现优于更复杂的替代模型。

Abstract: Time series of random objects, such as covariance matrices, probability distributions, and functional data, call for forecasting methods that do not rely on standard arithmetic operations. We introduce geodesic exponential smoothing, a generalization of exponential smoothing to time series in Hadamard spaces: the forecast level moves a fixed fraction of the way along the geodesic toward each new observation. The smoothing parameter is estimated by minimizing the average squared distance between observations and their forecasts. We further introduce an innovations mechanism under which each observation has conditional Fréchet mean equal to the current level, providing the metric-space analog of the innovations state-space model. In contrast to autoregressive models for object-valued time series, the framework involves a single scalar parameter, assumes no stationarity, and updates online in constant time per observation. Under this mechanism, we establish sample-path properties of the generative process via the quasilinearization available in Hadamard spaces, and prove almost-sure consistency of the smoothing-parameter estimator. Three real-data applications, spanning covariance-matrix, distributional, and functional time series, assess the forecasting performance of the method against structurally heavier alternatives.

</details>


### [197] [Gaussian Boundary Inference in a Hypergeometric Heavy-Tailed Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20393)
*Steve Lawford*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为一个高斯嵌套超几何分布族建立统计推断框架，该族在边界c=3/2处退化为标准正态分布，偏离边界时呈现带方向的厚尾变形；作者证明了该族的容许性，构造最小距离估计量并推导边界与内部渐近理论，且将方法推广到位置-尺度及稳健版本，应用于S&P 500日收益率验证其尾部拟合与风险分位数意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准正态分布对金融收益率等数据的对称厚尾偏离缺乏灵活的单参数建模工具，需要一种以正态分布为边界、能定向刻画厚尾程度并具备可检验性与可推断性的分布族。

Method: 作者引入高斯嵌套超几何分布族G_c(z)（含合流超几何函数_1F_1，边界c=3/2为标准正态，密度尾部g_c(z)~(c-3/2)|z|^{-3}），并给出等价的beta精度正态尺度混合表示；随后建立该族的容许性(admissibility)，构造最小距离估计量，推导原假设正态下内部的常规渐近与非标准的边界渐近，证明标准化拟合改进统计量收敛到混合分布1/2·δ_0+1/2·χ²_1；并将理论推广到插入式位置-尺度过程及稳健的中位数/IQR版本。

Result: 模拟显示检验具有准确的原假设水平(null size)和对厚尾备择的定向功效(directed power)。对S&P 500日收益率的无条件分析及GARCH(1,1)滤波后的分析表明，GARCH滤波显著减弱但并未消除检验所检测到的对称厚尾偏离，从而影响尾部拟合与风险分位数(risk quantiles)的估计。

Conclusion: 该超几何分布族为对称厚尾偏离正态提供了一类参数化、可定向检验且渐近性质完备的建模工具，边界非标准渐近和混合χ²极限分布使正态性检验得以实现；实证表明GARCH滤波对厚尾偏离的去除有限，对尾部风险量化具有实际意义。

Abstract: This paper develops inference for a Gaussian-nested hypergeometric family of distribution functions. The family \[ G_c(z) = \frac12 + z\,\frac{Γ(c-1/2)}{2\sqrt2\,Γ(c)}\,{}_1F_1\!\left(\frac12;c;-\frac{z^2}{2}\right),\quad c\ge\frac32, \] contains the standard normal distribution at the boundary $c=3/2$. Away from the boundary, the density has algebraic tail behaviour $g_c(z)\sim(c-3/2)|z|^{-3}$, so the parameter $c$ indexes a directed heavy-tailed deformation of the Gaussian law. The distribution also admits an equivalent beta-precision normal scale-mixture representation, in which the Gaussian boundary corresponds to degenerate unit precision. I establish the admissibility of the hypergeometric family, derive minimum-distance estimators, and obtain both regular interior asymptotics and nonstandard boundary asymptotics under the normal null. The standardised fit-improvement statistic converges to the mixture distribution $\tfrac12δ_0+\tfrac12χ_1^2$. I extend the theory to plug-in location-scale procedures, including a robust median/IQR version. Simulations document accurate null size and directed power against heavy-tailed alternatives. An application to daily S&P 500 returns, both unconditionally and after GARCH(1,1) filtering, illustrates the empirical implications for tail fitting and risk quantiles, and documents that GARCH filtering substantially reduces but does not eliminate the symmetric heavy-tailed departure detected by the test.

</details>


### [198] [Efficient computation of mixture confidence sequences in generalized linear models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20496)
*Claudia Di Caterina,Luigi Pace,Alessandra Salvan,Nicola Sartori*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对累积累积数据（批量/流式）场景下传统置信区间反复推断会导致高概率矛盾结论的问题，提出一种为广义线性模型回归系数计算“混合置信序列”（mixture confidence sequences）的简单高效策略，并通过模拟与美国国家汽车抽样系统的流式数据演示了其有效性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序贯/批量数据累积的情境下，若在不同样本量时反复计算经典置信区间，会产生高概率的相互矛盾推断；因此需要一种“任意时刻有效”（anytime-valid）的推断工具，以避免在使用流式或分批观测数据时得到误导性结论。

Method: 作者提出一种简单高效的策略，用于在序贯框架下为广义线性模型（GLM）的回归系数计算混合置信序列（mixture confidence sequences），即通过对参数先验进行混合（加权平均）构造出可在任意停止时刻统一有效的置信序列。

Result: 模拟实验表明该方法计算便捷，并凸显了在分批获得观测数据时采用“任意时刻有效”工具得出结论的重要性；在美国国家汽车抽样系统（NASS）流式数据的分析中，所提程序也展示了其有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合置信序列方法为广义线性模型回归系数提供了一种简单、计算高效且“任意时刻有效”的序贯推断方案，避免了经典置信区间在累积数据下反复计算所产生的矛盾，在流式数据应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Classical confidence intervals, when repeatedly obtained on accumulating data at different sample sizes, produce contradictory inferences with high probability. We propose a simple and efficient strategy for computing, instead, mixture confidence sequences for regression coefficients in generalized linear models under this sequential framework. Simulations demonstrate the computational convenience of our approach and the importance of drawing inferential conclusions based on these anytime-valid tools when observations become available in batches over time. The usefulness of the proposed procedure is also shown in the analysis of streaming data from the American National Automotive Sampling System.

</details>


### [199] [Sampling-Based Batch Sequential Design by Stein Variational Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20583)
*Penghui Fu,Xiaoxian Ding,Chunlin Ji,Jianhua Z. Huang,C. F. Jeff Wu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于采样的框架，利用 Stein 变分梯度下降（SVGD）将完全序贯（逐点）实验设计方法系统性地转换为批量序贯方法，并解决了约束设计区域与近均匀目标分布等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际实验设计问题需要跨阶段批量选择多个实验点，但现有文献大多聚焦于完全序贯（逐点）方法，缺乏将逐点方法有效推广到批量情形的通用手段。

Method: 提出一种基于采样的通用框架：将完全序贯方法的目标（效用）构造为合适的目标分布，并采用 Stein 变分梯度下降（SVGD）从该分布中高效采样一批点，从而在单个点效用与批次多样性之间取得平衡；同时针对约束设计区域和近均匀目标分布等问题对 SVGD 进行适配。

Result: 通过将该框架应用于多种最先进的完全序贯方法得到其批量版本，大量数值研究表明所提批量方法性能良好。

Conclusion: 该采样框架能够系统地将逐点序贯设计方法转化为批量序贯方法，借助 SVGD 兼顾效用与多样性，并成功处理约束区域与近均匀分布等难点，具有广泛的适用性和良好表现。

Abstract: Many real-world experimental design problems require a batch of experimental runs across stages, in which multiple points are selected and evaluated at each stage. However, most work in the design literature is focused on fully sequential (point-by-point) methods. This paper proposes a sampling-based framework to systematically convert a fully sequential method to a batch sequential method. In particular, Stein variational gradient descent (SVGD) is adapted to efficiently sample a batch of points from a properly constructed target distribution while balancing the individual utility and the batch diversity. We address challenges that arise in using SVGD for experimental designs, including constrained design regions and near-uniform target distributions. We apply the proposed method to obtain batch versions of the state-of-the-art fully sequential methods, and demonstrate their performance through extensive numerical studies.

</details>


### [200] [Conditional Independence Testing in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20772)
*Jieru Shi,Rajen D. Shah*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出通用时序协方差测度（GTCM），一种无模型的Granger因果检验方法。它通过将结果与暴露分别对Y和Z的联合历史做非线性回归，并基于残差样本协方差构建检验统计量，结合方差权重与数据自适应多项式滞后展开，在弱假设下控制I类错误并提升功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Granger因果检验依赖线性VAR模型，易受模型误设影响；而金融、神经科学、气候科学等应用中需要区分真实因果效应与由共同外部过程驱动的虚假关联。因此需要一个无模型、对模型误设稳健的检验方法。

Method: 文章将结果Y_{t+1}与暴露历史\bar X_t均对Y和Z的联合历史做非线性回归，取残差的样本协方差作为检验统计量（GTCM）。为应对异方差并提高对局部备择假设的功效，引入方差权重，并采用基于多项式滞后展开的数据自适应检验。I类错误控制仅需回归程序以足够快的速率估计条件均值，该速率慢到足以容纳非参数设定；在回归程序稳定且时间序列弱依赖的假设下，可无需分割样本而利用全部数据估计条件均值。

Result: 所提GTCM在相对弱的假设下实现I类错误控制，并通过方差加权和数据自适应多项式滞后展开提升了对局部备择假设的功效；在回归程序稳定与时间序列弱依赖条件下可完整利用数据集。

Conclusion: GTCM提供了一种无模型的Granger因果检验方法，对模型误设稳健、能有效控制I类错误并在局部备择下具有良好功效，适用于金融、神经科学、气候科学等领域中区分因果效应与虚假关联的决策需求。

Abstract: We consider the problem of testing Granger causality in time series, specifically, whether the future outcome $Y_{t+1}$ and the exposure history $\bar X_t$ are conditionally independent given the history of $\bar Y_t$ and a set of confounding variables $\bar Z_t$ up to time $t$. This testing procedure distinguishes true causal effects from associations driven by common external processes, supporting reliable decision-makings in applications such as finance, neuroscience, and climate science. While traditional approaches assume a linear vector autoregressive (VAR) model and are vulnerable to misspecification, we instead address a model-free version of the problem. We propose nonlinearly regressing both the outcome and exposure on the joint history of $Y$ and $Z$, and calculating a test statistic based on the sample covariance of residuals, we call the Generalised Temporal Covariance Measure (GTCM). To account for heteroscedasticity and improve power against local alternatives, we incorporate variance weights and employ a data-adaptive test based on polynomial lag expansions.The type I error control of the test relies on the relatively weak assumption that user-chosen regression procedures estimate conditional means at a sufficiently fast rate that is slow enough to accommodate nonparametric settings. By further assuming stability of the regression procedures and weak dependence in the time series, we can utilise the entire dataset to estimate the conditional means without splitting the time series into subsets.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种意图驱动的查询建议框架，通过双阶段优化（意图感知多样性建模 + 查询级信用分配）同时提升单个查询质量与候选列表的意图覆盖度，并在生产数据集的在线/离线实验中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式查询建议的核心挑战在于：既要保证候选列表（slate）中每个查询本身有用，又要让整个列表覆盖用户不同的搜索意图。现有方法难以同时兼顾查询级质量与列表级意图多样性。

Method: 提出意图驱动的查询建议框架，包含两阶段优化：第一阶段为意图感知多样性建模，构建意图对齐的监督微调（SFT）数据，并设计 Intent-Aware Diversity Reward 来优化意图覆盖度；第二阶段为查询级信用分配，将个别的质量信号路由到对应查询的 token，同时在候选列表层面共享多样性信号。

Result: 在大规模生产数据集上开展离线评估与在线 A/B 测试，结果显示点击率（CTR）、查询质量和意图覆盖率均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过意图驱动的双阶段优化，能够在提升单个查询质量的同时增强候选列表的意图多样性，有效改善用户体验与用户参与度。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [202] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大语言模型（LLM）压缩的分层课程学习（layer-wise curriculum learning）方法，通过将模型按层分段、由易到难地迁移知识，并引入特征缓存与多线程策略，在保持先进压缩性能的同时，将 GPU 显存占用与训练时间降低 50% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 压缩（如知识蒸馏/剪枝）在训练过程中面临计算开销大、GPU 显存占用高、训练时间长的问题，且存在逐层误差累积（cumulative error）现象，影响知识迁移的收敛速度与稳定性。作者希望设计一种更高效、更稳定的知识迁移方案，以降低压缩成本并提升性能。

Method: 作者提出分层课程学习框架用于 LLM 压缩：(1) 将整个模型按层划分为多个分段（segments），采用课程学习策略，从较易的优化任务开始、逐步过渡到更难的任务，实现教师模型向学生模型的知识迁移；(2) 基于对累积误差现象的理论分析，证明该方法能加速收敛并稳定知识迁移过程；(3) 提出带有多线程策略的特征缓存（feature caching）方法，以高效解决跨层的特征不对齐（feature misalignment）问题，最大化 GPU 利用率。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）的压缩性能；在 BERT 和 GPT-2 上，GPU 显存占用与训练时间均降低超过 50%。在相同的训练时长下，该方法在 LLaMA 系列和 Qwen 模型上的剪枝（pruning）表现优于其他方法，且具有更低的 GPU 显存占用。

Conclusion: 分层课程学习能够在保证先进压缩性能的同时，显著提升 LLM 压缩的计算效率，大幅降低 GPU 显存占用与训练时间，并在多种模型（BERT、GPT-2、LLaMA 系列、Qwen）上验证了其有效性与通用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>
