On the KoTaP panel of 1{,}754 Korean listed firms from 2011--2024, PaGNet is evaluated under a leakage-free, shared-hyperparameter protocol across four feature regimes. In the direct-proxy-lag-excluded FS1 regime and the tax-history-augmented FS2 regime, accrual targets (TSTA, TSDA) route stably to the LightGBM branch, where PaGNet raises explained variance over the strongest of six baselines by roughly $0.08$--$0.11$ on the primary split. GETR often leans toward the neural branch, while CETR exposes a validation--test branch-selection mismatch rather than a stable branch assignment. A panel-flatten control shows that most accrual gains come from observed multi-year base-panel values, with PaGNet's panel-aware representation adding a smaller but directionally consistent refinement. Rolling-origin analysis confirms stable accrual routing, bounds ETR diagnostics to split-specific behavior, and identifies a far-horizon split where supervised models underperform naive persistence. PaGNet is therefore best viewed not as a universally superior tabular learner, but as a proxy-aware panel model that combines competitive forecasting with explicit per-target branch-reliance reporting.

</details>


### [372] [Sequential Contextual Fit Predicts Human Behavioural and Neural Dynamics Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20179)
*Kun Sun,Rong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种可跨领域通用的"序列情境契合度"（SCF）指标，用于衡量当前信息状态与其近期情境的匹配程度，并证明较低的情境契合度能预测更长的处理时间、更大的情感/行为转变及更强的神经状态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类的感知、行动与决策都以序列方式展开，但现有的计算预测指标大多局限于特定领域（如语言中的惊奇度、强化学习中的预测误差等），缺乏一个能跨领域统一描述"当前状态与近期情境是否协调"的通用度量。

Method: 提出序列情境契合度（SCF），一种基于嵌入的度量：采用简单的"近因加权相似性核"（recency-weighted similarity kernel）计算当前信息状态与近期情境的匹配程度。该指标可应用于词汇、声音、视觉场景、情感状态、选择、动作及神经表征等多种模态/领域。

Result: 在语言加工、音乐诱发情绪、视听情绪脑电数据子集、赌博决策、人类活动识别及决策相关脑电等多个数据集中，较低的情境契合度均预测了更长的处理时间、更大的情感或行为转变以及更强的神经状态变化。且在控制了惊奇度、强化学习预测误差、声学变化、视觉变化和传感器变化等既有预测因子后，这些效应依然显著。

Conclusion: SCF 提供了一层通用的计算度量，可将"情境相容性"与行为加工及认知/神经状态转变动力学联系起来，为跨领域理解序列处理中的情境效应提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Human perception, action and decision making unfold in sequences, but computational predictors are often domain-specific. This study computes and tests sequential contextual fit (SCF), an embedding-based measure of how well a current information state matches its recent context. The metric uses a simple recency-weighted similarity kernel and can be applied to words, sounds, visual scenes, affective states, choices, actions and neural representations. Across language processing, music-evoked emotion, a subset of audiovisual emotion EEG data, gambling decisions, human activity recognition and decision-related EEG, lower contextual fit predicted longer processing times, larger affective or behavioural transitions and stronger neural-state changes. These effects remained after controlling for established predictors including surprisal, reinforcement-learning prediction error, acoustic change, visual change and sensor change. SCF therefore provides a computational measurement layer for relating contextual compatibility to behavioural processing and cognitive/neural state-transition dynamics.

</details>


### [373] [JointMatch: A Unified Heterogeneous Graph Neural Solver for Large-Scale Ride-Sharing Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20200)
*Kun Zhao,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JointMatch 是一个学习型框架，将共享出行的请求配对与车辆分配统一到单张稀疏图上用图神经网络一次性打分，既提升了收益又大幅加快了调度速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流学术方法将共享出行调度拆分为"先请求配对、后车辆分配"两个顺序匹配问题，并分别使用独立求解器。这种分解虽然计算上方便，但会损失收益且扩展性差，因为第一阶段在获知可用车辆之前就已确定拼车组合，导致决策次优。

Method: 提出 JointMatch，一个基于学习的框架，将请求配对与车辆分配统一在单张图上联合处理。图通过空间邻近性进行稀疏化，使其规模随车辆与请求数量呈线性增长（而非平方增长）；随后用一个图神经网络在一次前向传播中对所有候选决策进行打分。

Result: 在纽约市黄色出租车数据上，JointMatch 已同时超越经典 Blossom 启发式算法与忠实训练的两阶段 GNN 基线，且常常领先幅度很大；在城市规模（车队 10000 辆）下，每个调度周期的运行速度比两者快 20 倍以上。监督训练阶段弥补了大部分剩余收益差距，策略梯度微调则使训练模型与真实收益对齐。

Conclusion: 联合处理请求配对与车辆分配、并采用空间稀疏化和单次前向打分，能够在城市规模下同时实现更高的收益与显著更快的调度速度，验证了学习型统一框架在共享出行调度中的有效性。

Abstract: Ride-sharing platforms must continuously decide which open requests to bundle into shared trips and which idle vehicles should serve them. The dominant academic approach decomposes this into two sequential matching problems -- request pairing first, then vehicle assignment -- and applies a separate solver to each. This decomposition is convenient computationally but loses revenue and scales poorly because the first stage commits to ride bundles before the available vehicles are known. We propose JointMatch, a learning-based framework that handles request pairing and vehicle assignment together on a single graph. The graph is sparsified by spatial proximity so that its size grows linearly rather than quadratically with the number of vehicles and requests, and a graph neural network scores all candidate decisions in one forward pass. On the New York City Yellow Taxi data, the framework already exceeds both the classical Blossom heuristic and a faithfully-trained two-stage GNN baseline -- often by a wide margin -- and at city scale (fleet 10000) it runs more than $20\times$ faster per dispatch epoch than either. A supervised training stage closes most of the remaining revenue gap, and a policy-gradient fine-tune aligns the trained model with realised revenue.

</details>


### [374] [When AI Agents Commit: Cognitive Serializability Across Data, Evidence, Policy, and Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20261)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Typed Contract Transactions（TCT），一种面向自主智能体的事务处理机制。通过类型化依赖令牌区分内容完整性与适用性，并以可信中介捕获推理事物的输入值，保证在严格认知可串行化下，已提交效应存在串行顺序，且推导所用输入保持不变；在较弱的“效应兼容认知准入”下则允许对效应重新认证。TCT 融合了不可变版本化定义、密封信封、守卫先行提交、共提交收据、幂等授权终结等机制，实现零误差健全性与正向进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体从数据库读取、检索证据、策略、信念与委托授权中推演出具体“变更”（mutation），但这些输入可能在推理进行中持续变化。传统数据库隔离只对已提交事务排序，智能体事务处理只判定提案是否满足可执行合约，二者都无法为“变更”与其推导输入确立一个共同的有效时点，除非合约显式编码相关谓词，否则无法保证推演结果的正确性。

Method: 提出 TCT，核心机制包括：类型化依赖令牌（typed dependency tokens）区分“内容完整性”与“适用性”；可信中介（trusted mediation）捕获暴露给推理的值；严格认知可串行化（Strict Cognitive Serializability）要求已提交效应存在串行顺序及一个逻辑事件点，使推导所用值不变，栅栏持续到密封持久域实现的运行时事件；较弱的“效应兼容认知准入”（Effect-Compatible Cognitive Admission）对照同时持有的当前依赖向量与当前策略重新认证效应，而不声称序列化原始随机推导。实现上组合了不可变版本化可执行定义、注册表派生授权计划、密封信封（sealed envelopes）、守卫先行提交事务（guard-first commit）、封后信封与见证绑定的授予、共提交收据、幂等授予终结、收据驱动的认知协调；通过完整注册足迹与单一增长阶段诱导无环锁点排序。

Result: 给出可串行化条件，以及零误差健全性与正向进展的观察等价边界。伪造测试套件验证实现义务：原型阻止了所有注入的异常，平均提交开销增加 3.22 ms。

Conclusion: TCT 通过类型化依赖令牌、可信中介、密封信封与守卫先行事务等机制，为自主智能体在动态输入下的推导与变更确立了严格或较弱的正确性保证，并以可串行化条件与观察等价边界刻画其健全性与进展，实证上以约 3.22 ms 的平均提交开销实现了零异常。

Abstract: Autonomous agents derive concrete mutations from database reads, retrieved evidence, policy, beliefs, and delegated authority. Those inputs may change while reasoning is in progress. Database isolation orders the submitted transaction; agentic transaction processing determines whether a proposal satisfies an executable contract. Neither guarantee establishes a common valid point for the mutation and its derivation inputs unless the contract represents the relevant predicates. Typed dependency tokens distinguish content integrity from applicability, and trusted mediation captures the values exposed to reasoning. Under strict Cognitive Serializability, committed effects admit a serial order and a logical event at which every value exposed to derivation is unchanged. The fences last until the runtime event that realizes the sealed durability domain. The weaker Effect-Compatible Cognitive Admission recertifies an effect against a simultaneously held current dependency vector and current policy without claiming to serialize the original stochastic derivation. TCT combines immutable versioned executable definitions, registry-derived authority plans, sealed envelopes, guard-first commit transactions, post-seal envelope- and witness-bound grants, co-committed receipts, idempotent grant finalization, and receipt-driven epistemic reconciliation. Complete registered footprints and a single growing phase induce an acyclic lock-point order over local guards and incompatible external reservations. The corresponding results give serializability conditions and an observational-equivalence boundary for zero-error soundness and positive progress. A falsification suite tests the implementation obligations: the prototype prevented all injected anomalies and added 3.22 ms mean commit overhead.

</details>


### [375] [AI-Driven Real-Time Relay Optimisation in Smart Urban NR-V2X Networks via Learning-to-Optimise Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20271)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的学习优化（L2O）框架，用于NR-V2X网络中实时的多跳中继选择，以在RSU部署有限和复杂城市环境下提升车联网连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NR-V2X网络的可靠低延迟通信是智慧城市与工业4.0应用的基础需求，但路侧单元（RSU）部署有限且城市传播条件复杂，导致网联自动驾驶车辆（CAV）难以维持稳定连接，需要一种能实时优化的中继选择方案。

Method: 将车联网建模为图（节点为CAV与RSU，边编码无线链路特征），以离线混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解出的最优中继决策作为监督标签，训练一个边感知的带边特征的图同构网络（GINE）。

Result: 在真实城市数据集上的实验表明，该框架取得近最优的连通性能，恢复了最高11.3%的连通性增益，同时相比MILP将执行时间降低数个数量级（最高达100倍加速）。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了可扩展的实时网络控制，适用于智慧城市与工业4.0场景的部署。

Abstract: Reliable and low-latency communication is a fundamental requirement for smart city services and Industry 4.0 applications enabled by NR-V2X networks. However, limited Road-Side Unit (RSU) deployment and complex urban propagation conditions often prevent Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs) from maintaining stable connectivity. This paper proposes an AI-driven Learning-to-Optimise (L2O) framework based on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for real-time multi-hop relay selection in NR-V2X systems. The vehicular network is modelled as a graph, where nodes represent CAVs and RSUs, and edges encode radio-link characteristics. An offline Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) formulation provides optimal relay decisions used as supervision for training an edge-aware Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE). Extensive experiments on realistic urban datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves near-optimal connectivity performance, recovering up to 11.3% connectivity gain, while reducing execution time by orders of magnitude (up to 100 x speed-up) compared to MILP. The framework enables scalable and real-time network control, making it suitable for smart city and Industry 4.0 deployments.

</details>


### [376] [JEPA-WAM: Connecting Generated Visual Instructions to World Action Models through JEPA Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20277)
*Tianbin Liu,Jian Zhu,Taiyi Su,Jianjun Zhang,Chong Ma,Zitai Huang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JEPA-WAM 通过为文本指令生成一组随机视觉指令（任务完成图像）并利用冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器提取语义目标表征，以紧凑的目标 token 条件化视频与动作专家，从而显著提升世界动作模型（WAM）的指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 仅依赖文本指令时指令跟随能力受限，其根本原因在于机器人学习数据存在结构性不平衡：丰富的视觉-动作轨迹往往只配有稀疏且重复的语言标注，导致策略通过视觉上下文和运动规律来识别任务，而非真正理解指令本身。

Method: 提出 JEPA-WAM：① 使用现成的文生图生成器（无需训练）根据文本指令采样多张任务完成图像作为视觉指令；② 这些图像虽可能与当前场景在外观和布局上有差异，但在语义上与指令一致，充当视觉目标参考；③ 用冻结的 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器编码这些参考图，获得超越外观的任务级语义稠密表征；④ 将稠密目标表征压缩为紧凑的目标 token，通过交叉注意力条件化视频专家和动作专家。此外构建了一个真实机器人指令跟随基准，覆盖分布内、分布外场景和分布外指令三种设置。

Result: 在该基准上，JEPA-WAM 在分布内、分布外场景、分布外指令三种设置下分别取得 87.3%、74.5% 和 80.9% 的成功率，相比 π0 和 Fast-WAM 分别至少高出 10.0、27.3 和 14.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过为文本指令补充语义对齐的视觉指令，并以语义级目标表征条件化视频与动作专家，JEPA-WAM 有效缓解了语言标注稀疏造成的指令理解不足，显著提升了机器人的指令跟随能力并展现出更强的泛化性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong robotic manipulation capabilities by augmenting pretrained video generative models with action experts. However, current WAMs still show limited instruction-following ability when conditioned solely on text instructions. We argue that this limitation stems in part from a structural imbalance in robot-learning data: rich visual-action trajectories are often paired with sparse and repetitive language annotations, allowing policies to identify tasks from visual context and motion regularities rather than grounding the instruction itself. To address this limitation, we introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments each text instruction with a bank of stochastically generated visual instructions, providing diverse visual cues for instruction following. Specifically, JEPA-WAM uses an off-the-shelf text-to-image generator to sample multiple task-completion images conditioned on the text instruction, without training the generator. Although these generated images may differ from the current visual scene in appearance and layout, they remain semantically aligned with the instruction and serve as visual goal references. To focus on task-level semantics beyond appearance, we encode these references with a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. The resulting dense goal representations are compressed into compact goal tokens that condition both the video and action experts through cross-attention. We further construct a real-robot instruction-following benchmark covering in-distribution, out-of-distribution scene, and out-of-distribution instruction settings. On this benchmark, JEPA-WAM achieves success rates of 87.3%, 74.5%, and 80.9% in these three settings, outperforming π0 and Fast-WAM by at least 10.0, 27.3, and 14.5 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [377] [AgentPProf: Semantic Profiler for Long Horizon AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20301)
*Yusheng Zheng,Chaokun Chang,Yu Mao,Tianyuan Wu,Yuxi Huang,Tao Ma,Wenan Mao,Shuyi Cheng,Andi Quinn,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentPProf，一个面向智能体（agent）轨迹的语义化剖析器，通过语义操作栈和递归操作切分，将跨运行的 agent 活动聚合成 pprof 兼容的剖析结果，从而支持火焰图分析、资源归因、问题定位与成本优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI agent 往往长时间编排多用户、多工具、多系统资源的活动，开发者需要回答"失败发生在哪里、什么触发不安全后果、哪些任务消耗最多预算"等问题以提升质量、安全性与成本效率。但现有 agent 可观测性工具聚焦单次执行的调试与追踪，缺乏跨运行、长时间的剖析能力；且 agent 的责任主体是"任务意图"（如诊断认证、比较分支）而非代码路径，缺少稳定标识符可用于聚合，导致传统剖析方法难以直接迁移。

Method: 提出"语义操作栈模型"，将剖析思想适配到 agent 轨迹：用统一操作（uniform operations）表示所有活动，用操作栈替代运行时调用栈，实现不同粒度的层次化归因。基于"agent 任务占据连续区间并可分解为子任务"的观察，提出"递归操作切分"（recursive operation segmentation），在任务边界处递归拆分轨迹。最终实现 AgentPProf 剖析器，将 agent 轨迹聚合为 pprof 兼容的 profile，支持火焰图可视化与分析。

Result: AgentPProf 在 CodeTraceBench 上相对人工标注达到 0.764 的 B³ F1 分数；在三个问题定位基准上，剖析结果使 MAP（平均精度均值）最高提升 56%，且剖析开销处于实用水平，证明其能有效归因资源、定位问题并辅助降低 token 成本。

Conclusion: agent 可观测性需要的不只是调试，更是剖析。AgentPProf 通过语义操作栈与递归操作切分，成功将传统剖析范式扩展到 agent 轨迹，实现了跨运行聚合、层次化归因与问题定位，为大规模 agent 系统的质量、安全与成本优化提供了实用工具。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly orchestrate long-running activities with users, tools, and system resources for days and weeks. To improve agent quality, safety, and cost efficiency, developers need to determine where failures happen, what triggers unsafe effects, and which tasks consume the most budget, then optimize those tasks. In systems software, profiling answers similar questions by aggregating resource consumption and attributing it to responsible code paths to identify hotspots. Yet existing agent observability tools focus on per-execution debugging and tracing rather than cross-run, long term profiling, making these questions difficult to answer at scale. Agent observability needs profiling, not only debugging, but profiling agents is challenging: the responsible entities are task intent like diagnose authentication, compare branches rather than code paths, and lack stable identifiers for aggregation. We propose a semantic operation stack model that adapts profiling to agent trajectories. Uniform operations represent all activities, and operation stacks replace the runtime call stack, enabling hierarchical attribution at different granularities. We observe that an agent's task occupies a contiguous span and decomposes into subtasks, so we introduce recursive operation segmentation, which recursively splits trajectories at task boundaries. AgentPProf is a profiler that aggregates agent trajectories into pprof-compatible profiles, enabling flame graph visualization and analysis. AgentPProf reaches 0.764 $B^3$ F1 against human annotations on CodeTraceBench. On three problem-localization benchmarks, the profile raises MAP by up to 56%, demonstrating that it effectively attributes resources, locates problems, and helps optimize token cost at practical profiling cost. AgentPProf is available at https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/agentsight.

</details>


### [378] [Diagnose, Recover, Certify: Task Readiness under Hidden Dynamics Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20304)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"休眠动态漂移下的任务就绪"决策问题，并提出基于证据门控匹配脉冲传输的贝叶斯干预流程，在有限交互预算内实现故障定位与执行器有效性估计的耦合，从而在任务揭晓前完成可恢复策略选择与就绪认证，兼顾性能、置信度与安全回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的控制策略可能掩盖潜在动力学变化：执行器有效性下降时，若当前任务很少激励该执行器，故障可能不被察觉，但对未来尚未指定的任务却至关重要。这要求系统在任务揭晓前，用有限的、任务无关的交互预算完成主动故障诊断与恢复能力表征。

Method: 提出任务就绪决策框架，统一主动变化诊断与变化后控制恢复。核心方法为证据门控匹配脉冲传输（Evidence-Gated Matched-Pulse Transport），一种基于干预的贝叶斯流程，通过共享的匹配响应表示将故障定位与执行器有效性估计耦合，在保持诊断可靠性的同时将局部证据转化为恢复相关的后验不确定性；该不确定性再传播到任务条件下的策略选择与就绪认证，显式权衡期望性能、置信度与回退使用。

Result: 在覆盖多个仿真器的多样化休眠执行器基准上评估，协议将诊断与能力恢复分离，以就绪覆盖率、选择性风险、交互成本及回报进行评分，并识别出传输证据具有决定性作用的故障状态。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够在有限交互预算下可靠地完成故障定位与恢复能力估计，并通过证据传输将诊断不确定性转化为任务级就绪认证，实现部署决策中性能、置信度与安全回退的显式权衡。

Abstract: A deployed control policy can conceal consequential dynamics changes: an actuator may lose effectiveness without affecting the current task when the policy rarely excites it, despite being critical for a future task that has not yet been specified. We introduce task readiness under dormant dynamics drift, a decision problem that unifies active change diagnosis and post-change control recovery under a limited, task-agnostic interaction budget. An agent must identify whether and where local dynamics have changed, use a small number of informative interactions to characterize the change before downstream task identity is revealed, and subsequently provide each candidate task with either a recovered policy and a calibrated lower bound on its achievable return or an abstention decision to a safe fallback. We propose Evidence-Gated Matched-Pulse Transport, an intervention-based Bayesian procedure that couples fault localization with estimation of actuator effectiveness through a shared matched-response representation, thereby preserving diagnostic reliability while converting localized evidence into recovery-relevant uncertainty. This uncertainty is propagated to task-conditioned policy selection and readiness certification, enabling deployment decisions that explicitly trade off expected performance, confidence, and fallback use. We evaluate the resulting framework on a diverse suite of dormant-actuator benchmarks spanning multiple simulators, under a protocol that separates diagnosis from capability recovery, scores deployment by readiness coverage, selective risk, and interaction cost as well as return, and identifies the fault regimes in which transported evidence is decisive.

</details>


### [379] [NeuSOGA3D: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Explainable 3D Geometric Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20323)
*Qingde Li,Qingqi Hong,Zihan Li,Jie Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NeuSOGA3D，一种结合神经感知先验与符号几何推理的混合框架，将无序点云重建为可解释、CAD 兼容的显式符号几何表示（控制多边形、隐式样条场、截面、体积放样），并在 ModelNet40 全部 40 类上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经隐式方法虽重建精度高，但几何信息被编码在潜在表示中，缺乏可解释性，难以在工程工作流中复用。因此需要一种既保持重建能力、又能输出显式、结构化、可复用几何实体的方法。

Method: NeuSOGA3D 将点云投影到主正交平面，基于观测构造符号隐式样条表示，通过保形构造实体几何（CSG）操作融合生成粗略视觉外壳；再通过截面分解与偏保形样条（Partial Shape-Preserving Splines）进行体积重建，逐步将观测转化为控制多边形、隐式样条场、截面和体积放样等显式符号实体。

Result: 在 ModelNet40 全部 40 个类别的实验中，框架能够从多样化点云观测中恢复结构上有意义且 CAD 兼容的几何表示，验证了神经感知与符号几何推理相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: NeuSOGA3D 展示了将学习感知与符号几何推理相结合以实现可解释几何智能的潜力，为点云三维重建提供了更可解释、可复用的显式几何表示方案。

Abstract: Three-dimensional reconstruction from unorganized point clouds remains a challenging problem in computer vision, geometric modeling, and computer-aided design. While neural implicit methods achieve impressive reconstruction accuracy, geometry is typically encoded in latent representations that limit interpretability and reuse within engineering workflows.
  We present NeuSOGA3D (Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction in 3D), a hybrid framework that combines learned perceptual priors inherited from NeuSOGA with explicit symbolic geometric reasoning. The method projects point clouds onto principal orthographic planes, constructs symbolic implicit spline representations from the resulting observations, and fuses them through shape-preserving constructive solid geometry operations to generate a coarse visual hull. Additional geometric detail is recovered through cross-sectional decomposition and volumetric reconstruction using Partial Shape-Preserving Splines.
  Unlike conventional neural implicit approaches, NeuSOGA3D progressively transforms observations into explicit symbolic entities, including control polygons, implicit spline fields, cross-sections, and volumetric lofts. Experiments on all forty categories of the ModelNet40 benchmark demonstrate the ability of the framework to recover structurally meaningful and CAD-compatible geometric representations from diverse point-cloud observations. The results highlight the potential of combining learned perception with symbolic geometric reasoning for explainable geometric intelligence.

</details>


### [380] [Structured Four-Stage Legal Translation: From Natural-Language Traffic Rules to PROLOG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20334)
*May Myo Zin,Wachara Fungwacharakorn,Ken Satoh,Katsumi Nitta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化四阶段法律翻译框架（S4L→Prolog），将自然语言交通法规直接翻译为可执行的Prolog逻辑规则，在20条真实交通规则的基准测试中取得75%的正确形式化准确率，优于两种基线方法（NL→Prolog 60%、LE→Prolog 55%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通法规面向人类解读，依赖共享背景知识和灵活措辞，天然存在歧义、语境依赖和语义欠明确等问题，这与Prolog等计算推理引擎所要求的显式逻辑结构相冲突，因此需要自动化方法将法规可靠地翻译为逻辑形式。

Method: 作者评估了两种基线翻译方法（自然语言到Prolog NL→Prolog、逻辑英语到Prolog LE→Prolog），并提出S4L→Prolog框架。该框架在单一引导提示内依次完成四个阶段：语义角色提取、场景补全、逻辑映射和Prolog规则生成，无需人工干预即可将原始交通规则直接翻译为可执行逻辑。评估采用由20条真实交通规则构成的基准，从语法有效性、语义正确性和逻辑完整性三个维度进行评测，并进行定性分析。

Result: S4L→Prolog取得最高准确率，正确形式化75%的规则；NL→Prolog达到60%，LE→Prolog达到55%。定性分析表明，S4L在捕捉隐式因果关系、道义情态和例外结构方面比基线方法更可靠。

Conclusion: 结构化推理提示能够显著提升自然语言到逻辑翻译在法律及安全关键应用中的可靠性，S4L框架通过多阶段引导有效缓解了法规文本的歧义与语义欠明确问题。

Abstract: Traffic regulations are written for human interpretation and therefore rely on shared background knowledge and flexible phrasing, which inherently introduce ambiguity, context dependence, and semantic underspecification. These linguistic characteristics conflict with the precision required by computational reasoning engines such as Prolog, which demand explicit logical structure. This study evaluates two baseline translation approaches, Natural Language to Prolog ($NL\rightarrow Prolog$) and Logical English to Prolog ($LE\rightarrow Prolog$), and introduces a new reasoning-guided translation framework called Structured Four-Stage Legal Translation ($S4L\rightarrow Prolog$). The proposed S4L framework performs semantic role extraction, scene completion, logical mapping, and Prolog rule generation within a single guided prompt, enabling direct translation of raw traffic rules into executable logic without human intervention. A benchmark consisting of twenty real-world traffic rules was used to evaluate each approach in terms of syntactic validity, semantic correctness, and logical completeness. $S4L\rightarrow Prolog$ achieves the highest accuracy, correctly formalizing 75 percent of the rules, while $NL\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 60 percent and $LE\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 55 percent. Qualitative analysis further shows that S4L captures implicit causal relations, deontic modality, and exception structure more reliably than the baselines. These results demonstrate that structured reasoning prompts can substantially improve the reliability of natural-language-to-logic translation for legal and safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [381] [A Qualitative Model for Reasoning about Path and Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20349)
*Abhishek Jaiswal,Zoe Falomir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向积木拼图游戏 Camelot Jr. 的混合定性推理模型，用于培养儿童空间技能，并提供可解释的类人游戏指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理能力与 STEM 表现密切相关，而游戏是训练儿童这一能力的理想媒介。但要在游戏中实现类人辅导与指导，需要 AI 智能体能对空间事件进行常识推理，并生成可解释的反馈。定性推理（QR）模型因其符号化表示特性，恰好能胜任这一需求。

Method: 本文为 Camelot Jr. 积木拼图游戏设计了一个混合定性模型。该游戏要求玩家搭建多层桥梁来连接分处两座塔上的角色，玩家需保证平台稳定、规划路径并全部使用提供的积木。为应对该领域对精确物理的需求，模型整合了基于质心的数学稳定性逻辑，用以引导定性求解器进行推理。

Result: 所提出的混合定性模型能够在 Camelot Jr. 中完成空间推理，并将游戏状态转换为可解释的符号化反馈，从而支持类人玩家引导。

Conclusion: 该工作促进了 Camelot Jr. 中的空间技能训练，并为开发以人为本、可解释的游戏智能体作出了贡献。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning abilities correlate strongly with performance in STEM fields. Games offer a compelling medium for training these critical skills in developing children who have a natural proclivity for play. However, to facilitate human-like tutoring and player guidance, these games require an AI agent capable of making commonsense inferences from spatial events. Qualitative reasoning (QR) models appear to be a suitable framework for these application domains. As these models reason in symbolic representations, they can seamlessly translate game states into interpretable feedback for human-like player guidance. This paper introduces a hybrid qualitative model designed for Camelot Jr., a block-puzzle game that requires constructing multi-level bridges to connect two avatars stationed on separate towers. The game poses a challenge for the player, who must make platforms stable, plan their path, and ensure they use all the provided blocks. To handle the precise physics required by the domain, we integrate a mathematical center-of-mass stability logic to guide our qualitative solver. Our work facilitates spatial skill training in Camelot Jr. and contributes to the development of human-centric, explainable game-playing agents.

</details>


### [382] [Generating Heterogeneous 3D Geological Microstructures from 2D Images via a Stable Diffusion-Adversarial Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20358)
*Ali Aouf,Eric Laloy,Bart Rogiers,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合方法，将去噪扩散模型的稳定性与生成质量同对抗损失相结合，用于从2D切片图像重建黏土和水泥基材料的三维微观结构，无需3D真实数据即可训练，生成结果在相分数和结构描述符上与真实值高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏土和水泥基材料的三维微观结构模拟通常依赖昂贵、受限的3D成像。现有基于GAN的SliceGAN在均匀各向同性与各向异性体系上表现良好，但难以捕捉复杂非均质微观结构的细节，因此需要新的生成框架来从易获取的2D图像重建3D体积。

Method: 作者提出一种混合生成方法，借助去噪扩散模型的稳定性和生成质量；由于缺乏3D真实值（ground truth），将标准去噪损失替换为对抗损失，从而在实验中实现稳定训练，用于从2D切片生成不同复杂度的3D微观结构。

Result: 该模型能够生成不同复杂度的微观结构，切片伪影极少，且在相分数与结构描述符上与真实值高度一致，证明了方法对复杂非均质体系的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于去噪扩散模型与对抗损失的混合框架，可在无3D真实数据条件下，从2D图像高质量重建复杂非均质材料的3D微观结构，克服了SliceGAN在细节捕捉上的不足。

Abstract: Characterizing the physical properties of clay and cementitious materials matters across many fields, from materials science to geological waste disposal. Property simulation typically calls for 3D imaging, which is expensive, not always accessible, and technically limited for certain materials. Recent progress in deep generative models offers a way around this, reconstructing 3D volumes from the more easily acquired 2D images.
  Among GAN-based methods for 3D microstructure generation, SliceGAN has shown strong results for homogeneous isotropic and anisotropic systems. It struggles, however, to capture the finer detail of more complex heterogeneous microstructures, which motivates alternative generative frameworks.
  We introduce a hybrid approach that draws on the stability and generation quality of denoising diffusion models. Since no 3D ground truth is available, we replace the standard denoising loss with an adversarial loss, which yields a stable training process in our experiments. We show that the resulting model generates microstructures of varying complexity with minimal slice artefacts and close agreement with ground-truth phase fractions and structural descriptors.

</details>


### [383] [The Organization of Inference: Information, Resource Constraints, and AI Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20449)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过受控工作流实验考察AI生产中容量与任务信息在各阶段的分布如何决定推理的经济价值，发现扩展容量、工作流与信息结构共同塑造系统产能的利用方式与产出价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理的经济价值取决于容量（容量资源）与任务信息在AI生产各阶段之间的分布方式，而这一组织性维度此前缺乏系统实证检验；作者旨在揭示规划与执行分离、信息可得性等工作流设计如何影响任务成功率与资源利用效率。

Method: 在外部验证的软件工程任务上开展受控工作流实验，设置两个匹配的资源面板（逻辑token上限分别为12,000与24,000），比较直接执行与信息受限规划两种模式；另设严格只读规划实验，操控规划器能否看到任务issue。

Result: 直接执行在两个token上限下成功率均为59.6%；信息受限规划随上限提高从36.2%升至51.2%，规划劣势收窄15.0个百分点（95%任务簇bootstrap区间4.2–25.8）。只读规划中，12,000 token下可见issue比隐藏issue提升约16个百分点；任务知情规划在12,000 token下比直接执行低约10个百分点，在24,000 token下反超29.6个百分点。直接执行实际用量远低于任一上限，而规划工作流的绑定率从46.2%降至0.8%，下游执行占总量增加的89.9%。

Conclusion: 规模决定系统可用的容量，而工作流与信息结构塑造该容量的生产性价值；单纯增加容量并不能等同地提升所有工作流的产出，组织设计（规划与执行的分工、信息可见性）是放大推理经济价值的关键杠杆。

Abstract: The economic value of inference depends on how capacity and task information are distributed across stages of AI production. We study these organizational margins using controlled workflow experiments on externally verified software-engineering tasks. In two matched resource panels, direct execution records the same success rate of 59.6 percent at logical-token ceilings of 12,000 and 24,000, while success under information-constrained planning rises from 36.2 to 51.2 percent. The planning disadvantage narrows by 15.0 percentage points (95 percent task-cluster bootstrap interval: 4.2 to 25.8). A strict read-only planning campaign varies whether the planner sees the task issue. At 12,000 tokens, issue access raises success by about 16 percentage points over issue-hidden planning. Compared with direct execution, task-informed planning is about 10 points lower at 12,000 tokens; at 24,000 tokens, it shows a 29.6-point advantage. In the resource panels, direct execution uses substantially less than either ceiling, while the planning workflow's binding rate falls from 46.2 to 0.8 percent and downstream execution accounts for 89.9 percent of the increase in total use. Scale determines the capacity available to a system; workflow and information structure shape the productive value

</details>


### [384] [SkillAA: Attribution-Guided Skill-Graph Updating with Targeted Validation and Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20455)
*Ziqiao Shang,Ling-Yue Ge,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillAA 是一个面向冻结语言模型的结构化技能优化框架，通过统一的技能图表示，将失败归因导向对特定图对象的局部修复，并用 Local/Big Gates 进行变更筛选，在多个基准上取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部技能方法在失败时直接编辑技能，缺乏从观测到的失败到可编辑位置的系统化路由；同时现有技能图在语义边界、对象地址和拓扑依赖方面利用不足，导致技能检索、定向更新和范围验证能力受限。

Method: 提出 SkillAA（Skill Abductive Attribution）框架，将技能的可适用性、执行与组合统一表示在一张图中；通过对比成功与失败执行来定位候选修复并路由到具体图对象；仅更新选定的局部结构；并利用 Local Gate 和 Big Gate 对候选变更进行提交前筛选，实现图范围化的验证。

Result: 使用 gpt-5.6-sol 时，SkillAA 在 SearchQA、LiveMath、DocVQA 上分别达到 81.5%、66.7%、91.2%，并在所有主要设置中取得最高的平均成绩。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了归因引导的图编辑与图范围化验证的有效性，说明结构化、可定位、可验证的技能优化能显著提升冻结语言模型的性能。

Abstract: External skills provide domain procedures without parameter updates, but existing methods often edit skills directly from failed rollouts without structured routing from an observed failure to an editable location; existing skill graphs also underuse semantic boundaries, object addresses, and topological dependencies for skill retrieval, targeted updating, and scoped validation. We introduce SkillAA (Skill Abductive Attribution), a structured skill-optimization framework for frozen language models. It represents skill applicability, execution, and composition in a unified graph, allowing the same structure to support skill selection, attribution-guided repair, and update validation. SkillAA contrasts successful and failed executions to route candidate repairs to specific graph objects, updates only the selected local structure, and uses Local and Big Gates to screen candidate changes before commitment. With gpt-5.6-sol, SkillAA reaches 81.5%, 66.7%, and 91.2% on SearchQA, LiveMath, and DocVQA, respectively, and attains the highest observed mean in every main setting. These results support the utility of attribution-guided graph editing and graph-scoped validation.

</details>


### [385] [How Do Agent Harnesses Create Value? Planning Information and Release Control in Stateful LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20474)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体在供给规划引导下的三个组件（规划、执行、检查）对任务成功、错误接受率和成本的影响。通过两个零售实验和一个航空试点实验，比较固定规划与打乱文本，并评估只读终端验证器的效果。结果表明规划提升任务成功（尤其在复杂任务），验证器能以极低成本拦截错误输出，而组件的重要性取决于错误接受的损失权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体通常依赖供给规划来引导执行并检查完成情况，但规划内容的具体贡献及其与验证组件之间的权衡尚不清楚。作者旨在量化这些组件对成功、错误接受和成本的影响，以指导实际部署中资源的合理分配。

Method: 在两个零售实验和一个航空试点（τ²-bench）中，将预先编写的任务特定规划（Fixed）与字数匹配的乱序策略文本（Sham）进行对比，以隔离规划文本内容的贡献。同时引入只读终端验证器，在 265 个匹配单元上评估其对成功率和错误限制的影响，并分析在不同错误接受损失权重下各组件收益的相对大小。

Result: 在 265 个匹配单元中，Fixed 规划将经验证的成功率提高 7.17 个百分点（90% 任务聚类自助区间为 1.15–13.36 个百分点），收益集中在高复杂度任务。只读验证器拒绝了 61% 的零售 oracle-无效情节，同时错误拦截了 17% 的正确情节，每个情节额外成本不足一美分。在低错误接受损失下规划增益占主导；在高损失下验证器避免的误通过占主导，且单独的验证器几乎能获得完整规划加验证栈的全部防误通过收益，而成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 规划内容在复杂任务中切实提升智能体成功率，而验证器以极低成本有效拦截错误输出。各组件的重要性取决于错误接受的损失权重，在高损失场景下，独立的只读验证器性价比最高。

Abstract: Agent harnesses supply planning guidance, organize execution, and check completion. We study how these components affect success, erroneous acceptance, and cost in two Retail experiments and an Airline pilot in $τ^2$-bench. The primary comparison pairs prewritten task-specific plans (Fixed) with shuffled policy text matched in word count (Sham), isolating the contribution of guidance content. Across 265 matched cells, Fixed improves oracle-verified success by 7.17 percentage points (90\% task-clustered bootstrap interval, 1.15--13.36 points), with gains concentrated in higher-complexity tasks. A read-only terminal verifier rejects 61\% of Retail oracle-invalid episodes while withholding 17\% of correct ones, at less than one cent of additional cost per episode. Which component matters more depends on the loss assigned to erroneous acceptance: at low liability the planning gain dominates; at high liability the verifier's avoided false passes dominate---and a standalone verifier captures nearly all the false-pass benefit of the full planning-plus-verification stack at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [386] [SoL-Pi: Recursively Scaling Auto-Research Loops for Efficient Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20519)
*Haozhe Liu,Tian Ye,Sensen Gao,Qihang Cao,Yitong Li,Mingchen Zhuge,Duomin Wang,Ruihua Zhang,Ping Luo,Jiawang Bian,Lei Zhu,Ligeng Zhu,Enze Xie,Song Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受递归自我改进（RSI）启发的框架级方法 SoL-Pi，通过在大规模多样化环境中滚动执行自动化研究循环，发现可迁移的效率改进机制，在不牺牲性能的前提下将编码代理的 token 流量降低 44.7-49.0%、API 成本降低约三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理正从受监督的代码补全转向无人值守的全天候探索，工作形态从孤立预测扩展为推理、工具使用与反馈的长轨迹。token 效率因此成为扩展递归自我改进的关键瓶颈，亟需在框架层面自动发现可复用的效率优化。

Method: 采用 RSI 启发的框架层（harness layer）方法，在数量与多样性日益增加的环境中规模化滚动执行自动研究循环（auto-research loops），从中筛选出四类存活机制，构成 SoL-Pi：动作执行、上下文压缩、观测处理与委托阅读。

Result: 在 51 任务 EdgeBench 评测上，SoL-Pi 在 GPT-5.6 Sol 与 Opus 5 上性能与 Pi 相当，同时将记录的 token 流量降低 44.7-49.0%，API 成本降低约三分之一。估算每小时相对原生 Codex 与 Claude Code 框架节省 8.75-13.50 美元，相对 Pi 节省 4.36-5.71 美元。

Conclusion: 框架层的规模化自动化研究循环能够产出超越其开发场景的可复用改进，推动自动化框架发现走向生产级成果，验证了在 harness 层进行 token 效率优化的可行性与价值。

Abstract: As coding agents move from supervised code completion to unattended, around-the-clock exploration, their work expands from isolated predictions into long trajectories of reasoning, tool use, and feedback. Token efficiency therefore becomes important for scaling recursive self-improvement. We take an RSI-inspired approach at the harness layer, scaling auto-research loops across increasingly numerous and diverse environments for harness rollouts. At this scale, the process yields reusable improvements that transfer beyond their development setting, moving automated harness discovery toward production-level outcomes. Four mechanisms survive selection and form SoL-Pi, spanning action execution, context compaction, observation handling, and delegated reading. On the 51-task EdgeBench evaluation, SoL-Pi achieves performance comparable to Pi across GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 while reducing recorded token traffic by 44.7-49.0% and API cost by about one third. In other words, estimated hourly savings are \$8.75-\$13.50 relative to native Codex and Claude Code harnesses, and \$4.36-\$5.71 relative to Pi.

</details>


### [387] [FreqCondNorm: Towards Cross-domain Predictive Maintenance through a Frequency-Conditioned Transformer Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20535)
*Zaynab Raounak,Camille LHermine,Zhiguo Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FreqCondNorm，一种基于 Transformer 的预测性维护模型，通过 FiLM 式频率条件归一化层统一不同采样频率的时序数据，并在五个公开数据集上预训练，在故障诊断上取得显著迁移效果，但对剩余使用寿命预测无提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习预测性维护模型在跨机器和跨工况间的可迁移性差，尤其当标注数据稀缺且信号采样频率跨越五个数量级（1 Hz 至约 100 kHz）时，难以统一建模。

Method: 提出 FreqCondNorm，一种 Transformer 架构，引入 FiLM 风格的频率条件归一化层以统一异构时间序列；在 CWRU、MFPT、UOC18、PRONOSTIA、CMAPSS 五个公开预测性维护数据集上，采用掩码自编码与对比学习，并结合平衡域采样进行预训练。

Result: 在故障诊断任务上，CWRU 达到 99.2% 准确率（比 CNN 高 6.4 个百分点），MFPT 零样本准确率 82.1%，展现出跨采样频率的强迁移能力；但该方法未改善剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测。

Conclusion: FreqCondNorm 在跨采样频率的故障诊断迁移上表现突出，但未提升剩余使用寿命预测，表明预训练目标与 RUL 任务之间存在不匹配，值得未来进一步研究。

Abstract: Deep learning predictive maintenance models suffer from poor transferability across machines and operating conditions, especially when labelled data are scarce and signals span five orders of magnitude in sampling frequency (1 Hz to ~100 kHz). We propose FreqCondNorm, a Transformer-based architecture that introduces a FiLM-style frequency-conditioned normalization layer to unify heterogeneous time-series within a single model. The architecture is pretrained on five public predictive maintenance datasets (CWRU, MFPT, UOC18, PRONOSTIA, CMAPSS) using masked auto-encoding and contrastive learning with balanced domain sampling. On fault diagnosis, the model achieves 99.2% accuracy on CWRU (+6.4 pp over CNN) and 82.1% zero-shot accuracy on MFPT, demonstrating strong transfer across sampling frequencies. However, the approach does not improve remaining useful life prediction, suggesting a mismatch between pretraining and RUL objectives that warrants future investigation.

</details>


### [388] [Refuse, Decompose, Refresh: A Claim-Safe Protocol for Closed-Loop AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20538)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"声明安全"（claim-safe）的 AI 评估协议，针对闭环节系统中可复现评估仍可能支持错误声明的问题，通过拒绝（Refuse）、分解（Decompose）、刷新（Refresh）三个动作，并在聚合仿真器中实例化验证，证明将弃权纳入结果、分解报告与参考相对性处理分布漂移能限制错误声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 评估即使完全可复现，也可能支撑错误的结论，这一风险在闭环系统中尤为突出：策略决定了被访问的状态、可观测的组件，以及哪些失败会留下可测量的痕迹。因此需要一种方法，在证据支撑不足时克制声明，并把"通过/失败"的单一标签拆解为可核查的组件，同时避免把分布漂移误当作故障证据。

Method: 提出含三个动作的声明安全协议：①Refuse——当干净的参考流或匹配的运行时比较缺乏支撑时弃权；②Decompose——分别报告协议执行、运行中的错误准入（false admission）与结构性假设，而非一个 PASS/FAIL 标签；③Refresh——将分布漂移告警视为"使参考地图失效并重新计算"的请求，而非故障证据。在仅聚合的仿真器中实例化，含 24 个策略组件、三种需求机制、两类故障掩蔽家族，以及独立的开发种子与留出（heldout）种子；采用预注册留出集的 1,440 个案例与 21,600 个分区行，使用单侧精确 95% 上界、嵌套聚类的负对数似然差等统计方法，并记录漂移日志。

Result: 72 个机制-组件单元中仅 55 个被参考准入，其中 54/55 保持运行准入，弃权成为结果的一部分；稳定错误准入在 20 个代表性组件中为 0，在冻结的 0.20 规则下单侧精确 95% 上界为 0.1391；在已准入单元内，受影响干净流量的比例超过名义故障单元比例：跨 540 个单元-臂行、嵌套于 20 个组件簇中，单元减流量的负对数似然差为每行 0.1264 nats，组件簇 95% 区间为 [0.0593, 0.1918]；漂移日志显示"零假设"必须相对参考而言——干净故障零流在三种机制下分别触发 15/15、0/15、14/15 次告警，仅中间机制与冻结检测器参考匹配。

Conclusion: 本文不提供普适阈值，而是贡献一份可执行的契约，将可观测支撑、统计校准与合法声明联系在一起，证明通过弃权、分解报告和参考相对的漂移处理，可在可复现评估中有效约束错误声明。

Abstract: An AI evaluation can be perfectly reproducible and still support the wrong claim. This risk is acute in closed-loop systems: policy determines visited states, observable components, and which failures leave a measurable trace. We propose a claim-safe protocol with three actions. Refuse: abstain when a clean reference stream or matched runtime comparison lacks support. Decompose: report protocol execution, operational false admission, and structural hypotheses separately rather than as one PASS/FAIL label. Refresh: treat distribution-shift alarms as requests to invalidate and recompute a reference map, not as fault evidence. We instantiate the protocol in an aggregate-only simulator with 24 policy components, three demand regimes, two fault-mask families, and independent development and heldout seeds. The preregistered heldout contains 1,440 cases and 21,600 partition rows. Only 55/72 regime-component units were reference-admitted and 54/55 remained runtime-admitted, making abstention part of the result. Stable false admission was 0/20 represented components, with a one-sided exact 95% upper bound of 0.1391 under a frozen 0.20 rule. Within admitted units, affected clean traffic outpredicted nominal fault-cell fraction: across 540 unit-arm rows nested in 20 component clusters, the cell-minus-traffic negative-log-likelihood difference was 0.1264 nats per row, with a 95% component-cluster interval of [0.0593, 0.1918]. A drift log shows why "null" must be reference-relative: clean fault-null streams triggered 15/15, 0/15, and 14/15 alarms across three regimes, while only the middle regime matched the frozen detector reference. Rather than a universal threshold, we contribute an executable contract linking observable support, statistical calibration, and justified claims.

</details>


### [389] [Language-model groups overstate consensus when replaying human deliberation on a reasoning task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20543)
*Tengfei Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究用大语言模型（LLM）智能体群组重放人类沃森选择任务小组讨论，采用同一套打分代码，发现智能体群组比人类更易达成共识，且模拟共识并不代表集体正确性，揭示了用模拟群组估计人类协商结果的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全共识率常被当作集体认知的指标，但其数值高度依赖"参与"与"最终状态"的操作化定义；以往基于LLM模拟群组估计人类协商结果的研究缺乏打分口径明确的基准，容易夸大模拟与人类的一致性。

Method: 研究重放了100个留出（held-out）的人类沃森选择任务小组，构建匹配的LLM智能体群组：为每位参与者的讨论前答案植入一个"信念锚定"智能体，并用同一套代码对人类和智能体打分。在多种人类打分定义下估计共识率，并进行两项揭盲后的敏感性分析——基于提交的比较（n=98）与参与匹配比较（n=45），同时考察去除早停和去除可记忆答案的重参数化情形。

Result: 在不同人类打分定义下，共识率估计介于24.0%至57.0%之间；约五分之一的人类参与者从不发言，而智能体几乎总是发言。基于提交的比较中，聊天与推理两种模式分别产生34.0和43.9个百分点的差距；参与匹配比较中分别为34.1和44.4个百分点，两条互补路径的差距收敛在0.5个百分点以内。差距在没有早停和去除可记忆答案的重参数化下依然存在；推理模式下智能体群组几乎一致达成共识，且多数是错误答案。模拟共识并不追踪集体正确率，信念锚定的智能体群组是对人类群组结果分布的有偏估计量。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个打分口径明确的框架，用于评估模拟群组对人类协商结果的估计；结果显示LLM智能体群组在本任务情境下系统地高估共识且低关联正确性，因此不能将其共识率直接当作人类集体认知的有效替代指标。

Abstract: Full-consensus rates are often treated as indicators of collective cognition, yet depend on how participation and final states are operationalized. We replayed 100 held-out human Wason groups with matched large language model (LLM) agent groups, seeding one belief-anchored agent per participant's pre-discussion answer and scoring agents and people with the same code. Across human scoring definitions, estimates ranged from 24.0% to 57.0%; about one fifth of participants never posted, whereas agents almost always did. Agent groups remained more consensual in two post-unblinding sensitivity analyses: the submit-based comparison (n = 98) yielded gaps of 34.0 and 43.9 percentage points for chat and reasoning modes, and the participation-matched comparison (n = 45) yielded gaps of 34.1 and 44.4 points. These complementary routes reduced different measurement asymmetries yet converged within 0.5 percentage points. The gap persisted without early stopping and under a reparameterization removing the memorizable answer; reasoning-mode groups then agreed nearly unanimously, mostly on incorrect answers. Simulated consensus did not track collective accuracy, and belief-anchored agent groups were biased estimators of the human group-outcome distribution in this setting. These analyses provide a scoring-explicit basis for assessing simulated-group estimates of human deliberative outcomes.

</details>


### [390] [Limits of Confidence in Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20581)
*Russ Webb,Amitis Shidani,Alice Bizeul,Dan Busbridge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从理论上论证离散扩散（remasking 与 uniform-state 采样器）每步同时写多个位置时，只有当被写位置在已固定 token 条件下彼此独立时，该步才与训练分布匹配；并通过合成任务 ScanAndAdd 验证依赖组被置信度排序整体写入，导致生成分布总变差达采样噪声底限的 29 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散采样器每步基于逐位置分布选择并写入多个位置，但在像素、音素、词语等领域中 token 之间存在内在依赖关系。作者希望阐明这种逐位置独立的采样方式无法表达 token 间的依赖，从而揭示其理论与实际的分布失配问题。

Method: 作者从理论上分析：某一步要与训练分布匹配，当且仅当被写位置在已固定 token 条件下条件独立；证明逐位置分布的乘积无法匹配依赖组，且逐位置边际分布不足以判断组内是否存在依赖（两组联合分布可有相同边际但取值组合不同）。此外，在联合分布具有闭式解的合成任务 ScanAndAdd 上，验证置信度排序写入的每个由两个及以上未确定位置组成的组都是依赖的，并测量生成分布的总变差及其相对采样噪声底限的倍数。

Result: 在 ScanAndAdd 上，置信度排序写入的每个由两个及以上未确定位置组成的组均为依赖组；测得生成分布的总变差为采样噪声底限的 29 倍，而逐样本指标达到 1.0。

Conclusion: 逐位置独立采样与序列 token 间的内在依赖相冲突，导致离散扩散生成分布显著偏离训练分布；依赖组的整体写入和逐位置边际对依赖性的不敏感是造成失配的根源。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion, including remasking and uniform-state samplers, generate a sequence by writing multiple token positions per step, drawing each from a per-position distribution and choosing which positions to write from those same distributions. For domains of general interest (pixels, phonemes, or words) there are inherent dependencies between tokens. We show that a step matches the training distribution only when the positions it writes are conditionally independent given the tokens already fixed, that no product of per-position distributions can match a dependent group, and that per-position distributions do not determine whether a group is dependent: two joint distributions can have identical per-position marginals while differing in which combinations of values occur. On ScanAndAdd, a synthetic task whose joint distribution is available in closed form, we verify that every group of two or more undetermined positions a confidence ranking writes is dependent, and measure the generated distribution to be $29\times$ the sampling-noise floor total variation while per-sample metrics are $1.0$.

</details>


### [391] [PAA: The Probabilistic Allen Algebra: A Generative and Complete Probabilistic Extension of Allen's Interval Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20634)
*Julian Eggert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率化Allen代数(PAA)，通过为时间点和区间建立高斯/截断高斯分布，将13种时序关系定义为同一概率空间中由线性不等式诱导的多元高斯正交概率，并用容忍带使接触关系获得正测度，从而推导而非假设Allen分类学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen区间代数的13种基本关系是基于精确区间边界的二值谓词，无法处理来自自然语言、感知、数据库或不确定历史中带有不确定性的时间信息，也无法表达"就在之前""大致在期间"这类具有分级(graded)含义的表述。

Method: 将时间点建模为高斯分布，区间由高斯中点加截断高斯时长构成；把每种关系定义为公共概率空间中的边界排序谓词——点-点关系归结为误差函数，点-区间与区间-区间关系归结为线性不等式诱导的多元高斯正交(orthant)概率；通过容忍带(tolerance band)使meets、starts、finishes、equals等接触关系获得正测度，容忍趋于零时恢复经典Allen代数，形成真正的划分。

Result: 在单一容忍条件下13种关系构成真正划分；粗粒度谓词(先后、重叠、包含)是叶关系的并集且概率为叶之和，区间坍缩为点时13种关系先降为5种再降为3种，层级结构保持不变；每个关系可进一步分解为符合CIDOC CRM思想的相关性感知时序原语；代数具有尺度不变性，并能区分"shortly before"这类分级表达与接触关系。

Conclusion: 所提概率化Allen代数是生成式且完备的扩展，从分布推导关系概率而非人为赋值，统一处理了13种关系及其层级与退化，全部结果经蒙特卡洛验证，并以开源、经过测试的Python包发布。

Abstract: Allen's interval algebra is a qualitative calculus for temporal relations, but its thirteen base relations are crisp predicates over exact interval boundaries. This is inadequate for temporal information from language, perception, databases, or uncertain histories, where times, durations, and boundaries are uncertain and expressions such as "just before" or "roughly during" have graded meaning. We develop the probabilistic Allen algebra (PAA): a generative and complete extension in which relation probabilities are derived from distributions over interval boundaries rather than assigned as scores. Time points are Gaussian; intervals have Gaussian midpoints and truncated-Gaussian durations. Every relation is a boundary-ordering predicate in one common probability space: point-point relations reduce to error functions, and point-interval and interval-interval relations to multivariate Gaussian orthant probabilities induced by linear inequalities. Contact relations (meets, starts, finishes, equals) receive positive measure through a tolerance band, and under a single tolerance the thirteen relations form a true partition that recovers crisp Allen as the tolerance vanishes. The construction derives Allen's taxonomy rather than positing it: coarse predicates such as precedence, overlap, and containment are unions of leaves whose probabilities are leaf sums, and this hierarchy is preserved as intervals collapse to points and thirteen relations reduce to five and then three. Each relation further decomposes into correlation-aware temporal primitives in the spirit of CIDOC CRM. The algebra is scale-invariant and separates graded expressions such as "shortly before" from contact relations. All results are Monte-Carlo validated and shipped as an open, tested Python package.

</details>


### [392] [Ownership in AI-Assisted Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20658)
*Megan Wei,Melanie Subbiah,Audrey Lee,Annya Dahmani,Dave Edwards,Helen Edwards,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一项探索性质性调查，探究人们使用AI完成任务时"拥有感/作者感"何时得以保留或丧失，发现拥有感取决于协作过程（主动引导、迭代、改写可保留，仅批准建议会失去），并提出促进人类作者感的AI设计研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI融入日常任务，需要审视当机器共同参与产出时，人们对自身工作的拥有感和贡献感会发生什么变化。作者旨在理解"与AI共同完成的工作何时仍感觉属于自己"，为设计支持人类作者感的AI提供依据。

Method: 采用探索性质性调查：让参与者描述两项近期自选的、与AI共同完成的任务——一项让他们感觉属于自己，另一项没有这种感觉；随后对回答进行定性分析，归纳出影响拥有感的因素。

Result: 发现拥有感取决于协作过程：仅批准AI建议会让人"失去所有权"，而主动引导、迭代或改写则有助于保留拥有感；当人们拥有项目的愿景但非具体执行时也能保有高度拥有感，即使任务离开了AI无法完成；个人声音的丧失和对输出缺乏理解都会侵蚀拥有感；AI使用意愿的披露往往与实际自豪感或拥有感脱钩，而受社群规范和"功劳被抹去"的恐惧所塑造。

Conclusion: 作者基于上述发现提出若干研究方向，旨在推动AI发展以支持人们对自己生活与工作的作者感。

Abstract: When does work done with AI still feel like ours? As AI becomes woven into everyday tasks, we must examine what happens to our sense of ownership and contribution when a machine shares in producing what we make. We report an exploratory qualitative survey in which participants were asked to describe two recent, self-selected tasks completed with AI: one that felt like their own and one that did not. We find that felt ownership depends on the process of collaboration: people disown work when they merely approve AI's suggestions, but retain ownership when they lead, iterate, or rewrite. Ownership can also extend to settings where people own the vision for a project but not the execution; respondents reported high ownership on tasks they could not have completed without AI. Loss of personal voice and a lack of comprehension of the output both erode ownership. Finally, willingness to disclose AI use is often decoupled from actual pride or ownership, and instead shaped by community norms and fear of credit erasure. We propose several research directions as a result of these findings to promote AI development that supports people's sense of authorship over their own lives.

</details>


### [393] [Deep Noir: Autonomous Steering Discovery via Architectural Chronometry in Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20722)
*Frank E. Bobe,Gregory D. Vetaw,Darshan W. Bryner,Matthew G. Cook,Jose L. Salas-Vernis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Deep Noir 是一个利用 Logit Lens 收敛性和因果头级归因来自动发现 LLM 激活引导（activation steering）最优参数（位置与强度）的框架，在垃圾邮件过滤和情感分类任务上显著提升性能，并揭示了引导强度与提示注入攻击脆弱性之间的单调递增关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导虽能在推理时修改 LLM 行为，但“在哪里引导、引导多强”目前依赖人工调参，缺乏自动化和机制层面的依据。作者希望用机制可解释性手段自动发现可跨任务、跨架构泛化的干预点，并量化引导带来的安全风险。

Method: 提出 Deep Noir 框架，结合两种机制分析技术：(1) Logit Lens 收敛性——衡量各层隐状态经线性读出后对目标标签的预测能力，用于定位适合干预的层；(2) 因果头级归因——识别对目标任务起关键作用的注意力头。两者共同自动确定引导层与引导强度的最优参数。实验覆盖 3 种规模、9 个模型（1B×3、2-3B×2、7-9B×4 共四种架构），在垃圾邮件检测（spam）和 SST-2 情感分类上验证，并与 RepE（含/不含头掩码）对比。

Result: 在垃圾邮件任务上，1B 模型平均提升 16.7 个百分点（标准差 4.7，39 次运行），7-9B 模型在四种架构上提升 21 至 42 个百分点；SST-2 上实现 13.1 个百分点提升且零代码改动。在情感任务上，不含头掩码的 RepE 未能超越基线，而 Deep Noir 对所有模型均有提升（p < 0.01）。此外发现引导会形成可预测的提示注入攻击面，其脆弱性随引导强度单调增加，对部署引导式分类器的智能体系统具有警示意义。

Conclusion: 机制层面的可解释性定位（Logit Lens + 因果归因）能够自动发现跨任务、跨架构泛化的激活引导干预点，带来稳健且显著的性能提升；同时，引导强度越高，提示注入攻击面越脆弱，部署时应权衡性能增益与安全风险。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies LLM behavior at inference time, but identifying where and how strongly to steer remains manual. We introduce Deep Noir, a framework that uses Logit Lens convergence and causal head-level attribution to autonomously discover optimal steering parameters. Across three scales (1B x 3, 2-3B x 2, and 7-9B x 4), our engine achieves 16.7 percentage-point improvement on spam at 1B (standard deviation 4.7; 39 runs), with gains increasing to 21 to 42 percentage points at 7-9B across four architectures. On SST-2 sentiment, it achieves a 13.1 percentage-point improvement with zero code changes. Mechanistic grounding enables automated discovery of intervention points that generalize across tasks and architectures. On sentiment, RepE without head masking fails to improve over baseline, while Deep Noir improves all models (p less than 0.01). We further show that steering creates a predictable prompt-injection attack surface whose vulnerability increases monotonically with steering magnitude. This finding is relevant to agent systems deploying steered classifiers.

</details>


### [394] [Q&A on Any Spreadsheet Requires Interpreting Its Grid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20732)
*Zofia Smoleń*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单元格语义标注的表格切分框架，提升了LLM驱动RAG系统中表格块的语义可解释性与答案生成质量，但指出单元格分类存在根本上限，主张未来应将二维非结构化表格直接降维展平为一维文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格本质上是二维非结构化数据，具有连续关系和近乎无限的潜在单元格角色。现有方法依赖分类模型将单元格归入有限、预定义类别，无法完整捕捉这种结构细微之处，构成电子表格到LLM的瓶颈，制约RAG系统的答案生成效果。

Method: 提出一种新颖框架，利用单元格角色标注（cell role annotation）将任意电子表格切分为具有可解释性的文本块，以语义标注增强块的可解释性，从而通过更丰富的上下文辅助LLM答案生成。

Result: 该框架在性能上超越了当前最先进方法，但面临"硬性天花板"——其改进体现在答案生成而非检索准确率上。作者论证即使达到人类级标注水平，离散单元格分类仍无法完美表征电子表格的连续结构与无限潜在角色。

Conclusion: 解决电子表格到LLM瓶颈需要超越离散的单元格分类，学界应发展降维技术，将二维非结构化表格直接展平为一维非结构化文本，使文本块更易被下游RAG解释和生成。

Abstract: Semantic cell annotation improves chunking interpretability for spreadsheets in LLM-driven RAG systems, aiding answer generation through enriched context rather than improved retrieval accuracy. We propose a novel framework of splitting any spreadsheet into interpretable chunks using cell role annotation. Our framework beats the state of the art, yet it faces a hard ceiling. Spreadsheets are fundamentally two-dimensional unstructured data with continuous relationships and infinite potential cell roles. Because classification models are restricted to finite, pre-defined classes, they cannot perfectly capture this structural nuance, even with human-level annotation. We show that addressing the spreadsheet-to-LLM bottleneck requires moving beyond discrete cell classification. Instead, the field must develop dimensionality-reduction techniques to directly flatten 2D unstructured spreadsheets into 1D unstructured text. Text chunks would be easier for downstream RAG to interpret and generate from.

</details>


### [395] [RAFT: A Stateful Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20754)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Xiaowen Wang,Anupma Sharan,Zhengyi Chen,Chenyu Diana Zhang,Shanshan Yang,Chittibabu Pacharu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAFT 是一个有状态的检索增强框架，将历史支持工单抽象为定向时间线条目链，并在条目级别检索，从而在故障排查的每个阶段都提升案例命中率，超越了普通 RAG 和 GraphRAG 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业客服中的有效故障排查代理需要从相似历史案例中检索可操作的指导，但现有 RAG 系统将支持工单视为静态文档，忽略了其多阶段、有状态的特性，导致无法利用案例的中间状态与进展轨迹进行匹配。

Method: RAFT 将有状态的 RAG 框架应用于故障排查代理：将每个已关闭的历史案例抽象为一条由时间线条目组成的有向链，并在条目（entry）级别进行检索，匹配活跃案例的中间状态并返回锚定在该匹配状态上的父案例轨迹；可选地通过一个案例级图连接案例，采用可配置的相似度表示。

Result: 在直接评估检索层（无需生产部署）时，RAFT 在案例进展的每个阶段都提升了 Case Hit，相对最强基线的提升具有统计显著性。由于公开的多阶段故障排查数据极其稀缺，作者构建了基于 Microsoft Learn Windows Server 文档的合成基准，并辅以带有人工重复标签的真实 Apache Jira 问题集；Jira 结果表明该优势可迁移到真实案例历史。

Conclusion: RAFT 通过状态化、条目级检索及可选的案例级图，显著改善了故障排查场景下的检索效果，且优势具有统计显著性并能迁移至真实数据；作者已公开基准、实现与 Apache Jira 评估集。

Abstract: Effective troubleshooting agents in enterprise customer support depend on retrieving actionable guidance from similar historical cases, yet existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat support cases as static documents and overlook their multi-stage, stateful nature. We introduce RAFT (Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents), a stateful RAG framework that abstracts each closed historical case into a directed chain of timeline entries and retrieves at the entry level, surfacing cases whose intermediate states match the active case and returning the parent-case trajectory anchored at the matched state; an optional case-level graph links cases through a configurable similarity representation. We evaluate this retrieval layer directly, which, unlike evaluating a full agent system, requires no production deployment. Because public multi-stage troubleshooting data is extremely rare, we pair a synthetic benchmark built from Microsoft Learn Windows Server documentation with real Apache Jira issues carrying human-created duplicate labels. RAFT improves Case Hit over vanilla RAG and GraphRAG baselines at every stage of case progress, with statistically significant gains over the strongest baseline; the Jira results provide directional evidence that the advantage transfers to real case histories. We release our benchmark, implementation, and the Apache Jira evaluation set.

</details>


### [396] [An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804)
*Run-Ze Fan,Zihao Zhang,Simin Ma,Yebowen Hu,Shouju Wang,Kaiqiang Song,Fei Liu,Hamed Zamani,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过固定执行循环、单独变换规划、动作空间与上下文管理三个组件，在四款模型、SWE-Bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上评估了 176 组匹配设置，系统揭示各组件对编码代理性能与成本的影响，并提出模型与预算感知的编程框架设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将编码框架作为整体系统来评估，无法厘清其中各组件（规划、动作空间、上下文管理）各自的贡献，导致难以进行组件级对比和针对性优化。

Method: 作者构建一个执行循环固定、仅变化规划、动作空间与上下文管理三个组件的轻量编码框架，在 SWE-Bench Verified 与 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上对四款模型开展 176 组匹配设置评估，覆盖五种上下文管理策略、四种上下文窗口预算，以及对规划和动作空间的定向消融，并辅以轨迹级分析。

Result: 上下文管理的价值随窗口预算收紧而增大，主要收益来自防止上下文溢出故障；先做基于规则的省略、再做 LLM 摘要的组合在效率上最优，而让省略内容可恢复的机制模型很少使用且无精度增益。规划对弱模型是精度支架，对强模型则成为成本节约手段且精度变化不大。预定义工具对 bash 能力弱的模型提升明显，而 bash 强的模型仅用 bash 接口即可有效工作且成本显著更低，尤其在命令行密集型任务上。轨迹级分析显示：上下文管理延长执行轨迹但不改变代理行为，规划改变轨迹停止的位置，动作空间改变代码编写的粒度。

Conclusion: 各框架组件对编码代理的影响呈现出模型依赖与预算依赖的特征，应据此进行模型与预算感知的框架设计；本研究同时提供了一个模块化框架，可用于评估未来的框架组件。

Abstract: Coding harnesses shape how autonomous coding agents translate model capabilities into long-horizon software-engineering performance, yet existing work typically evaluates harnesses as monolithic systems, leaving the effectiveness of individual components unclear. To enable component-level comparisons, we study this question with a lightweight coding harness whose execution loop is fixed while three components are varied: planning, action space, and context management. Across four models evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, we evaluate 176 matched settings spanning five context-management strategies, four context-window budgets, and targeted ablations of planning and action space. We find that: (1) Context management becomes increasingly valuable as the context-window budget tightens, with most of its benefit coming from preventing context-overflow failures. (2) Staging rule-based elision before LLM-based summarization provides the strongest overall efficiency among the context-management strategies, whereas making elided content recoverable adds machinery that models rarely use and yields no accuracy gain. (3) Planning shifts from an accuracy scaffold for weaker models to a cost saver for stronger models, with little change in accuracy. (4) Predefined tools improve performance for models with weaker bash proficiency, whereas bash-capable models can operate effectively with a bash-only interface and achieve substantially lower cost, especially on command-line-centric tasks. Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [397] [Federated Soft Clustering via Generalized Total Variation Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19202)
*Shamsiiat Abdurakhmanova,Alexander Jung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习网络中的联邦软聚类问题，各设备持有私有数据集并拟合个性化高斯混合模型(GMM)。通过广义全变差最小化(GTVMin)以图正则项耦合各节点的局部最大似然问题，并比较了三种模型差异度量（平方欧氏距离、蒙特卡洛近似KL散度、闭式MMD），全部用同步投影梯度优化，其中MMD具有收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习网络中，各设备数据私密且异构，需要在不直接共享数据的前提下实现协作式软聚类。GTVMin框架中模型间差异度量的选择是关键设计决策：基于参数的距离需要分量匹配，而直接比较模型分布的距离（KL散度、MMD）无需匹配，更能适应数据异构性。

Method: 采用广义全变差最小化(GTVMin)，将各节点的局部最大似然问题通过图正则项耦合。比较三种差异度量：①模型参数间的平方欧氏距离（需分量匹配）；②蒙特卡洛近似的KL散度；③闭式最大均值差异(MMD)。三种GTVMin实例均用同步投影梯度更新优化；对光滑的MMD实例给出了到平稳点的收敛性保证。

Result: 刻画了三种方法的计算成本，并评估了它们对数据异构性的鲁棒性。MMD度量因闭式解与光滑性兼具收敛性保证；KL散度与MMD因无需分量匹配而在数据异构场景下表现更稳健。

Conclusion: 直接比较模型分布差异的度量（MMD与KL散度）无需分量匹配，比基于参数的平方欧氏距离更适合异构联邦学习场景；其中MMD兼具闭式形式与光滑性，可获得收敛性保证，是更具吸引力的选择。

Abstract: We study federated soft clustering over federated learning (FL) networks of devices that each hold a private local dataset and fit a personalized Gaussian mixture model (GMM). Generalized total variation minimization (GTVMin) couples the local maximum likelihood problems through a graph regularizer that penalizes a discrepancy between the models of connected nodes. The choice of discrepancy measure is a key design decision: we compare a squared Euclidean distance between model parameters, which requires component matching, with two measures that compare the local model distributions directly and hence need no matching: a Monte-Carlo approximated Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and a closed-form maximum mean discrepancy (MMD). All three resulting GTVMin instances are optimized by synchronous projected gradient updates; for the smooth MMD instance we provide a convergence guarantee to stationary points. We characterize their computational cost and evaluate their robustness to data heterogeneity.

</details>


### [398] [Learning Submanifolds for Subsequent Inference on Random Dot Product Graphs, Part 1: Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19357)
*Michael W. Trosset,Carey E. Priebe*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向随机点积图的受限推断框架，利用未知低维支撑流形上的潜位置，并通过 Isomap 半监督规则学习该流形，使决策风险随辅助数据量增加收敛至预言规则的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多随机点积图模型中，节点的潜位置通常位于一个未知的低维支撑流形上。传统推断方法未能充分利用这种几何结构，导致推断效率受限。作者希望借助辅助数据学习该支撑流形，从而改进一般决策问题的推断效果。

Method: 提出一种受限推断框架：对一般决策问题设计半监督决策规则，利用辅助数据学习支撑流形。具体使用 Isomap 流形学习程序，在观测图上构造低维欧氏表示；在此低维空间中，一个等距不变函数将点的构型映射到决策动作。

Result: 随着取自未知支撑流形的辅助数据量增大，半监督规则的风险收敛到预言规则（oracle rule）的风险；该预言规则依赖于能从支撑流形中提取的最大低维欧氏结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的半监督受限推断规则在大样本辅助数据下渐近等价于利用理想低维结构的预言规则，具有良好的理论性质；示例、应用与模拟研究将在后续论文中给出。

Abstract: We propose a framework for restricted inference on random dot product graphs whose latent positions lie on an unknown low-dimensional support manifold. For general decision problems, we propose semisupervised decision rules that use auxiliary data to learn the support manifold. Specifically, our rules use the Isomap manifold learning procedure to construct a low-dimensional Euclidean representation of the observed graph, in which space an isometrically invariant function maps configurations of points to actions. We study the behavior of the proposed rules as the quantity of auxiliary data sampled from the unknown support manifold increases. We show that, as the auxiliary sample size increases, the risk of the semisupervised rule converges to the risk of an oracle rule that relies on the maximal amount of low-dimensional Euclidean structure that can be extracted from the support manifold. Examples, applications, and simulation studies are deferred to a sequel.

</details>


### [399] [Null importance: Disentangling relevance for interpretable machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19511)
*Garvesh Raskutti,Kris Sankaran,Jiaxin Ye*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于“零重要性”（null importance）的统一框架，用于区分特征重要性这一术语下不同的“相关性”概念，并厘清科学问题、数据/模型假设与评估方法三者之间的关系，从而阐明特征重要性分析所能支持的具体结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “特征重要性”在可解释机器学习中被广泛使用，但“重要性”一词其实涵盖了几种本质不同的相关性概念（如边际/条件统计相关、预测风险、函数不变性、因果效应），若不加区分会导致歧义甚至错误结论。

Method: 作者以总体层面的“零重要性”为核心，形式化地刻画“在特定相关性概念下某特征无关”的含义。具体做法包括：定义多类标准零重要性概念并说明其回答的不同科学问题；建立零重要性概念之间重合的充分条件，并给出条件不成立时发散的反例；刻画不同方法族各自针对的零概念，以及其零重要性统计量何时能识别这些目标；最后通过覆盖特征依赖、冗余、非线性、隐特征等现象的模拟实验，以及图像与多组学数据的案例研究进行实证验证。

Result: 不同零重要性概念在不同条件下既可能重合也可能发散；不同特征重要性方法族对应不同的零概念，仅在特定条件下其统计量才能正确识别目标零概念。在算法公平性（不同公平标准对应不同零重要性）和基因组扰动建模（不同相关性概念导致对模型所学内容的结论不同）两个应用中，这种区分尤为关键。模拟与案例研究为这些理论区分及其实际后果提供了经验证据。

Conclusion: 该框架为关联科学问题、数据生成假设与算法提供了一套统一的统计语言，明确了特征重要性分析所能支持的结论范围，有助于避免因混淆不同“重要性”概念而产生的误判。

Abstract: Feature importance is central to interpretable machine learning, but the term "importance" encompasses several fundamentally different notions of relevance. We develop a unified perspective based on null importance: a population-level characterization of when a feature is irrelevant under a specified notion of relevance. We consider standard notions of null importance arising from marginal and conditional statistical relevance, predictive risk, functional invariance, and causal effects, and show how these notions answer different scientific questions. We illustrate the framework in two applications in which the distinction is particularly consequential: algorithmic fairness, where common fairness criteria correspond to different notions of null importance, and genomic perturbation modeling, where different notions of relevance lead to different conclusions about what a prediction model has learned. The framework connects three aspects of feature analysis: the scientific question defining relevance, the data and model assumptions that shape how different null notions relate, and the methods used to assess importance. We establish sufficient conditions under which null notions coincide and give counterexamples showing how they diverge when those conditions fail. We then characterize which nulls different method families target and when their zero-importance statistics identify those targets. Finally, simulations spanning feature dependence, redundancy, nonlinearity, hidden features and other standard phenomena, along with case studies on image and multiomics data, provide empirical evidence for these theoretical distinctions and their practical consequences. Taken together, these results provide a common statistical language for relating scientific questions, data-generating assumptions, and algorithms, and clarify the conclusions that feature-importance analyses can support.

</details>


### [400] [Next-token functional estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19529)
*Milind Nakul,Vidya Muthukumar,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 leave-a-window-out（留窗口法）估计器，用于在时间相依数据下估计“下一个样本”相关的泛函（如惊奇概率、最小距离尾概率、分类器测试误差），并给出参数速率的误差上界与针对惊奇概率的极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 leave-one-out（留一法）在时间相依（temporal dependence）情形下不相合，无法可靠估计基于未观测终点的泛函，而现有方法缺乏对强相依随机过程的理论保证。

Method: 提出 leave-a-window-out 估计器：在每个索引后删除长度为 τ 的窗口再构造经验测度，τ=1 时退化为 leave-one-out。理论分析基于平稳 β-混合且具有 Marton 耦合的随机过程，证明误差以参数速率收敛。

Result: 在自然假设下，估计误差以参数速率衰减，适用于任意平稳 β-混合且具有 Marton 耦合的过程；并给出混合马尔可夫链上估计惊奇概率的紧极小极大下界。模拟表明在马尔可夫链、滑动平均与自回归过程中，所提方法在 leave-one-out 与加常数基线失效的多种情形下仍有效。

Conclusion: leave-a-window-out 估计器在大类随机过程上实现了参数速率的一致估计，理论上优于留一法并匹配极小极大下界，具有广泛适用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Suppose we observe the first $n$ points of a sequence of random variables having length $n+1$, and wish to estimate a functional of the unobserved final point and the empirical measure of the $n$ observed training points. Such next-token functionals include the probability that the next token is novel (also known as the surprise probability), the tail probability of the minimum distance between the next token and training points, and the test error of a classifier trained on the observed points. All of these quantities are classically estimated by the leave-one-out method, which is inconsistent under temporal dependence. We propose a leave-a-window-out estimator, which deletes a window of length $τ$ after each index before forming the empirical measure and reduces to leave-one-out at $τ= 1$. Under natural assumptions, we show that the error of our estimator decays at a parametric rate for any stationary $β$-mixing process that also admits a Marton coupling. Our results thus cover several natural functionals on a large class of stochastic processes. We complement these upper bounds with a sharp minimax lower bound for estimating the surprise probability on mixing Markov chains. Simulations on Markov chains, moving-average processes, and autoregressive processes show that our estimator succeeds in many scenarios where leave-one-out and add-constant baselines fail.

</details>


### [401] [Error bounds in Sobolev norms for approximations with norm constrained ReLU neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19937)
*Xianjun Li,Yunfei Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究带路径范数约束的ReLU神经网络对Sobolev函数的近似能力，将已有的一致逼近结果推广到Sobolev范数（W^{1,p}范数）度量下的逼近。对浅层网络给出依赖宽度W与路径范数界K的误差界，对深层网络则去除了光滑性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明了光滑函数可被路径范数有界的ReLU网络在一致范数意义下良好逼近，但一致逼近结果无法刻画导数的逼近质量。作者希望将该理论推广到Sobolev范数（W^{1,p}范数），从而同时控制函数值及其一阶导数的逼近误差，并探究光滑性指数n的约束条件。

Method: 以ReLU网络为载体，施加路径范数上限K的约束，分析宽度W、深度L的网络对W^{n,p}空间中函数的W^{1,p}范数逼近误差。对浅层网络（L=1），在光滑性指数满足n<s=(d+3)/2（d为输入维数）的条件下推导误差界；对深层网络，通过足够大的宽度W与深度L，消除对光滑性指数的限制。

Result: 浅层网络（L=1）的近似误差界为O(max{W^{-(n-1)/d}, K^{-(n-1)/(s-n)}})，其中s=(d+3)/2，且要求n<s；当宽度W与深度L充分大时，深层网络的近似误差界为O(K^{-(n-1)/(d+d/p+1)})，不再受光滑性指数限制。

Conclusion: 本文将路径范数约束下ReLU网络的逼近理论从一致范数推广到Sobolev范数，说明浅层网络在光滑性受限时可同时逼近函数及其导数，而深层网络能够去除光滑性限制，在W^{1,p}范数下以路径范数K的多项式衰减速率逼近W^{n,p}中的函数。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that smooth functions can be well approximated by ReLU neural networks with path norm constraint on the weights. We extend these results from uniform approximation to approximation in Sobolev norm. Specifically, we analyze how well Sobolev functions in $W^{n,p}$ can be approximated by neural networks with width $W$, depth $L$ and path norm bounded by $K$, when the approximation error is measured in the $W^{1,p}$-norm. For shallow networks with depth $L=1$, we derive the approximation error bound $\mathcal{O}(\max\{W^{-(n-1)/d}, K^{-(n-1)/(s-n)}\})$, when the smoothness index satisfies $n<s=(d+3)/2$ and the input is $d$-dimensional. For deep networks, we remove the restriction on the smoothness by showing that the approximation bound $\mathcal{O}(K^{-(n-1)/(d+d/p+1)})$ holds if the width $W$ and depth $L$ are sufficiently large.

</details>


### [402] [Model-based Bootstrap for Offline Policy Evaluation in Tabular Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20389)
*Weiwei Wang,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu,Bingyi Jing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的Bootstrap框架，用于有限时域、时间非齐次马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中离线策略评估（OPE）的不确定性量化，通过从估计的MDP中重新生成轨迹来获得更灵活、更高效、且具有有限样本有效性的置信区间与方差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险强化学习应用中，离线策略评估（OPE）不仅需要点估计，还需要可靠的不确定性量化（置信区间、方差估计）以支持安全与风险感知的决策。现有方法在鲁棒性、可扩展性或有限样本有效性方面存在不足，且经典Bootstrap方法依赖完整回合的重采样，难以适应多样的离线数据格式。

Method: 提出基于模型的Bootstrap框架，针对有限时域、时间非齐次MDP。与传统重采样完整回合不同，该方法从估计的MDP中重新生成轨迹，从而能兼容完整轨迹、转移级观测和轨迹片段等多种离线数据格式，并提升有限样本统计效率。文中还建立了Bootstrap分布一致性、渐近有效置信区间以及目标策略价值的一致方差估计。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，该方法能准确刻画OPE估计量的抽样分布，在多数场景下产生更紧的置信区间和更准确的方差估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于模型的Bootstrap统一框架，为离线策略评估提供了可靠、灵活且有限样本有效的不确定性量化手段，在有限时域时间非齐次MDP设定下具有理论保障和实证优势。

Abstract: Offline policy evaluation (OPE) is crucial in high-stakes reinforcement learning applications, where new policies must be assessed reliably before deployment. In such settings, point estimates alone are insufficient; principled uncertainty quantification, such as confidence intervals and variance estimates, is essential for safe and risk-aware decision-making. A comprehensive way to unify these tasks is to estimate the sampling distribution of the evaluation error. Existing approaches, however, often suffer from limited robustness, scalability, or finite-sample validity. In this paper, we propose a model-based bootstrap framework for uncertainty quantification of OPE in finite-horizon, time-inhomogeneous Markov decision processes (MDPs). Unlike classical bootstrap methods that rely on resampling complete episodes, the proposed method regenerates trajectories from an estimated MDP and can therefore accommodate a much broader range of offline data formats, including complete trajectories, transition-level observations, and trajectory fragments. This flexibility further improves finite-sample statistical efficiency. We establish bootstrap distributional consistency, asymptotically valid confidence intervals, and consistent variance estimation for the target policy value. Extensive simulations show that the proposed method accurately captures the sampling distribution of the OPE estimator, yielding tighter confidence intervals and more accurate variance estimates in most settings.

</details>


### [403] [Online Supervised Dimension Reduction with Random Features: Diagnostics and Computational Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20454)
*Zhenlin Yao,Wei Xiong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文区分了"优化光谱目标到精确"与"恢复真实总体子空间/获得更好预测表示"这两个概念，并针对在线核监督主成分分析（OKSPCA）进行了系统诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督谱目标的精确优化并不必然产生准确的总体子空间或更好的预测表示，需要厘清估计量、精确经验子空间与实际迭代轨迹三者在统计与计算上的区别。

Method: OKSPCA 将有限随机特征坐标下的中心化交叉矩与 Adam 式正交基更新相结合；论文给出固定映射一致性、集中性与扰动结果描述估计量及其精确子空间，并通过"同目标比较"评估实际迭代轨迹。在六个预测基准上比较追踪器与精确经验目标，分析直接分类排序模型与保存的中间状态，进行受控样本量研究，并在不同数值服务工作负载中对比精确按需计算与薄 SVD/Adam 的成本。

Result: 替换追踪器为精确经验目标后两类回归缺陷几乎不变；直接分类排序模型平均捕获几乎全部终端目标能量，但保存的中间状态存在显著几何偏差；样本量研究进一步将经验准确率与总体恢复分离。在测试的分类设置中精确按需计算更快，而在部分稠密宽回归请求中 Adam 相对全薄 SVD 服务节省时间，同时几何误差持续存在。

Conclusion: 这些诊断限制了仅以终端优化精度为依据的解释，并将数值成本与质量、秩覆盖和新鲜度区分开来；它们既未证明实际追踪器的收敛性，也未证明基可用性带来的预测或部署收益。

Abstract: Accurate optimization of a supervised spectral objective need not produce an accurate population subspace or a better predictive representation. We investigate these distinctions for Online Kernel Supervised Principal Component Analysis (OKSPCA), which combines a centered cross-moment in finite random-feature coordinates with an Adam-style orthonormal basis update for an established objective. Fixed-map consistency, concentration and perturbation results describe the estimator and its exact subspace; same-target comparisons then assess the practical iterate separately. Across six predictive benchmarks, performance depends on the declared pipeline: replacing the tracker with the exact empirical target leaves the two regression deficits largely unchanged. Direct classification-rank models capture nearly all terminal objective energy on average, but a saved intermediate state exhibits substantial geometric deviation; a controlled sample-size study further separates empirical accuracy from population recovery. In distinct numerical-service workloads, exact on-request computation is faster in the tested classification settings, whereas Adam saves time relative to the tested full thin-SVD service for some dense wider-regression requests, alongside persistent geometric error. These diagnostics limit explanations based solely on terminal optimization accuracy and distinguish numerical cost from quality, rank coverage and freshness; they establish neither practical-tracker convergence nor predictive or deployment benefits from basis availability.

</details>


### [404] [TAP Accuracy Below the Fluctuation Scale and Universal Posterior Geometry in Spherical Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20577)
*Jingbo Liu,Zhiyuan Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在成比例增长的高维设定下研究球形线性模型的贝叶斯最优估计，于定量的Marchenko–Pastur谱正则条件下证明全温度TAP逼近并刻画后验几何；给出自由能与TAP最优值之间的O_P(p^{-1})差距、O_P(p^{-1/2})的（尖锐）波动尺度、后验均值邻近性，以及由岭估计量确定的数据依赖带外后验质量的指数阶上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维球形线性模型在贝叶斯推断与统计物理中具有核心地位，但现有TAP（Thouless–Anderson–Palmer）逼近结果常依赖较强的设计矩阵假设（如独立同分布元素、高斯性）或缺乏定量误差控制。本文旨在更弱、更一般的定量Marchenko–Pastur谱正则条件下，建立高维球形模型的定量全温度TAP逼近，并精确刻画后验分布的几何集中性质。

Method: 作者在样本量与维数成比例增长（有限纵横比）的框架下，仅假设设计矩阵满足定量的Marchenko–Pastur谱正则条件（不要求元素独立，也不对奇异向量施加条件；该条件被标准化且有有限四阶矩的i.i.d.设计满足）。通过构造自然的有限纵横比TAP泛函，利用谱正则性证明定量全温度TAP逼近；进而分析归一化球形自由能与TAP最优值之差、其与非随机确定等价量的偏差；并对由岭估计量确定的带构造球帽下界，研究后验质量的指数阶上下界。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 归一化球形自由能与TAP最优值之差为O_P(p^{-1})，二者与其显式确定等价量之差均为O_P(p^{-1/2})，且该波动尺度是尖锐的；(2) 对所有全局TAP极大点一致地，到球形后验均值的归一化平方欧氏距离为O_P(p^{-1})；(3) 由岭估计量确定的数据依赖带外后验质量具有尖锐的指数阶：对足够小的带宽ε，质量的对数至多为-cpε²+O_P(1)；对每个固定的几何可容宽度，球帽构造给出匹配的指数阶下界；对任意满足ε_p≫p^{-1/2}的带宽序列，相应带捕获渐近全部后验质量。

Conclusion: 本文在最弱且可验证的Marchenko–Pastur谱正则条件下建立了高维球形线性模型的定量全温度TAP逼近与精确的后验几何刻画，显著放宽了对设计矩阵的假设，并给出了自由能波动、后验均值距离及带外质量指数阶等尖锐且匹配的上下界，为高维贝叶斯推断与平均场理论提供了严格的定量基础。

Abstract: We study the Bayes-optimal spherical linear model as the ambient dimension and sample size grow proportionally, under a quantitative Marchenko--Pastur spectral-regularity condition on the design. This condition is satisfied by normalized i.i.d. designs with standardized entries of finite fourth moment, but does not require entrywise independence or impose conditions on the singular vectors. Under this condition, we prove a quantitative all-temperature TAP approximation and characterize the posterior geometry. For the natural finite-aspect-ratio TAP functional, the normalized spherical free energy and the TAP optimum differ by $O_P(p^{-1})$. Each is within $O_P(p^{-1/2})$ of its explicit deterministic equivalent, and this fluctuation scale is sharp. Uniformly over all global TAP maximizers, the normalized squared Euclidean distance to the spherical posterior mean is $O_P(p^{-1})$. We also prove that the posterior mass outside a data-dependent band determined by the ridge estimator has sharp exponential order. More precisely, uniformly over sufficiently small band widths $\varepsilon$, the logarithm of this mass is at most $-cp\varepsilon^2+O_P(1)$. For every fixed geometrically admissible width, a spherical-cap construction gives a matching exponential-order lower bound on this mass. For every deterministic sequence of widths $\varepsilon_p\gg p^{-1/2}$, the corresponding bands capture asymptotically all posterior mass.

</details>


### [405] [Prediction-Powered Smoothing and Validation for Disaggregated AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20758)
*Sho Kawano,Zehang Richard Li,Paul A. Parker*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出预测驱动平滑（PP-S）及分类法增强版（PP-TS）等小区域估计方法，用于在标签稀缺时对各领域进行准确的点估计与区间估计，并给出近似无偏的基于设计的交叉验证分数用于选择直接或平滑估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统评估需要分领域（如基准任务类型、对话类型）的细粒度评估，而穷举测试成本高昂，评估只能依赖有限的标注样本；直接估计量（含预测驱动推断 PPI）在标签稀少的领域精度不足，需要借用强度的小区域估计与可靠的估计量选择方法。

Method: 将评估集视为有限总体，借鉴小区域估计思想构建一体化估计与验证流程：估计端提出预测驱动平滑 PP-S（对每个领域的 PPI 估计拟合贝叶斯模型）及其跨报告分类法借用强度的扩展 PP-TS；验证端推导一个近似无偏、基于设计（design-based）的交叉验证分数，用于在直接估计量与平滑估计量之间做选择，并估计所选估计量的误差。

Result: 在两个数据集上验证：一个可验证评分的精选基准和一个由人工评分的部署代理流量数据集，且每个结果均被观测。提出的估计量在点估计和区间估计上均优于直接估计量，覆盖率接近名义水平；在相同采样预算下，所提选择分数与独立验证样本的选择效果相当，并且能更准确地估计所选估计量的误差。

Conclusion: 通过小区域估计方法对预测驱动推断进行平滑（PP-S/PP-TS），并配合基于设计的交叉验证分数，能够在标签稀缺的分领域评估中显著提升点估计与区间估计精度、获得近名义覆盖率，并以更高效率实现可靠的估计量选择与误差估计。

Abstract: Evaluating an AI system requires disaggregated assessment, as performance varies across domains such as benchmark task types or conversation types in deployed agents. Exhaustive testing is expensive, so evaluation rests on a sample of labeled units. We treat the evaluation set as a finite population and seek accurate point and interval estimates of each domain mean. Direct estimators, including prediction-powered inference (PPI), use only a domain's own labels and are imprecise where labels are few. Small area estimation addresses this problem, and we build on it to develop an integrated workflow for estimation and validation. For estimation, we propose prediction-powered smoothing (PP-S), a Bayesian model fit to each domain's prediction-powered estimate, with an extension that borrows strength across a reporting taxonomy (PP-TS). For validation, we derive a new, approximately unbiased design-based cross-validation score for choosing among direct and smoothed estimators. We study a curated benchmark with verifiable grading and deployed agent traffic graded by humans, each with every outcome observed. In both, the proposed estimators improve on the direct estimators in point and interval estimation, with near-nominal coverage. At the same sampling budget, our score selects as well as an independent validation sample does and estimates the selected estimator's error far more accurately.

</details>
